Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一框架，将离线开发与在线影响桥接起来，用于Nubank（拥有1亿+用户）的客户支持AI代理。该框架整合了结构化上下文工程、人工参与的提示迭代、基于LLM裁判的评估（含GEPA优化）以及从构思到生产的验证。在五个生产部署中，AI代理的客户满意度持续提升，其中卡交付场景的AI交易净推荐值提升37个百分点，自助服务率提升29个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM能力的快速提升使得AI代理在广泛任务中变得可行，但构建面向客户的生产级AI代理需要在评估方法、上下文工程、训练和在线测量等方面进行协调，而这些关键支柱通常孤立开发，导致部署后才暴露出盲点。

Method: 提出统一框架，包含：(1)针对客户支持代理的结构化上下文工程；(2)系统性的人机协同提示迭代；(3)基于LLM裁判的严格评估，含测量一致性信度和GEPA优化；(4)从构思到生产的验证流程。

Result: 在卡交付、债务管理、信用额度支持、卡管理和产品解释五个领域部署。卡交付场景中，大规模A/B测试显示AI交易净推荐值提升37个百分点，自助服务率提升29个百分点。离线模拟指标与在线结果强相关，AI满意度接近专家人类代理水平。

Conclusion: 评估流水线质量直接决定迭代速度，以评估驱动开发能可靠预测生产影响。统一框架可有效桥接离线开发与在线部署的差距。

Abstract: The rapid rise in LLM capabilities has made AI agents increasingly viable across a broad range of tasks. Among the most promising applications is building production-ready customer-facing agents, a challenge that demands coordinated excellence in evaluation methodology, context engineering, training, and online measurement. Yet these critical pillars are typically developed in isolation, creating blind spots that only surface after deployment.
  In this paper, we present a unified framework that bridges offline development with online impact for customer support AI agents at Nubank, a company with 100M+ users. Our approach integrates several key components: (1) structured context engineering tailored to customer support agents, (2) systematic human-in-the-loop prompt iteration, (3) rigorous LLM judge evaluation with measured inter-rater agreement and GEPA optimization for consistency, and (4) ideation-to-production validation.
  A central insight is that evaluation-pipeline quality directly determines iteration velocity. We present results from five production deployments spanning distinct domains: card delivery, debt management, credit-limit support, card management, and product explanation. These deployments deliver consistent customer-satisfaction gains while substantially accelerating iteration. In our card-delivery deployment, large-scale A/B testing yields a 37 percentage-point improvement in AI transactional Net Promoter Score and a 29 percentage-point gain in self-service rate over prior agent variants, alongside a strong correlation between offline simulation metrics and online outcomes, demonstrating that eval-driven development reliably predicts production impact. On most use cases, AI satisfaction reaches within a few percentage points of expert human agents.

</details>


### [103] [PerspectiveGap: A Benchmark for Multi-Agent Orchestration Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08878)
*Youran Sun,Xingyu Ren,Kejia Zhang,Xinpeng Liu,Jiaxuan Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 PerspectiveGap 基准，用于评估 LLM 在多智能体系统中编排提示的能力。在 110 个场景和 27 个模型中，最佳模型 GPT-5.5 的通过率仅为 62.0%，平均通过率仅 14.9%，表明多智能体编排提示是一项尚未被充分评估的关键能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的 LLM 应用正从单智能体工作流转向多智能体系统，但当前模型难以确定每个子智能体需要知道什么信息。缺乏评估 LLM 编排多智能体提示能力的标准化基准。

Method: 构建 PerspectiveGap 基准，包含 110 个场景，通过角色片段分配和自由形式提示写作两种任务格式评估，场景按 10 种拓扑结构组织，基于 Prompt Economy 原则（以循环为中心的编排，最大化效用、最小化角色与工程开销）。对 10 家公司的 27 个商业模型进行实验。

Result: GPT-5.5 以 62.0% 通过率显著领先所有竞品；Opus 4.7 在编排提示方面表现薄弱（尽管编码能力强）；模型平均组合通过率仅 14.9%；平均总体信息泄露率 246.5%（GPT-5.5 为 49.1%）。

Conclusion: 多智能体编排提示是一项独特且未被充分评估的能力，PerspectiveGap 为系统化评估和提升该能力提供了基础。

Abstract: Real-world LLM applications are moving beyond single-agent workflows toward orchestrated multi-agent systems, yet current models still struggle to determine what each sub-agent needs to know. To measure this, we introduce PerspectiveGap, a benchmark for evaluating LLMs' ability to compose orchestration prompts for multi-agent systems. PerspectiveGap contains 110 scenarios, each evaluated through two distractor-mixed task formats: role-fragment assignment and free-form prompt writing. These scenarios are organized into 10 topologies, which are distilled from the authors' real-world engineering practice and framed by the Prompt Economy principle: building loop-centered orchestrations that maximize utility with minimal role and engineering overhead. In experiments with 27 commercial models from 10 companies, GPT-5.5 substantially outperforms all competitors, whereas Opus 4.7 shows a notable weakness in orchestration prompting despite its strong coding performance. Nevertheless, PerspectiveGap remains challenging: the evaluated models achieve an average combined pass rate of only 14.9\% (GPT-5.5 62.0\%) and an average overall leakage rate of 246.5\% (a per-scenario information leak-event count, not a proportion; GPT-5.5 49.1\%). These findings suggest that multi-agent orchestration prompting is a distinct and under-evaluated capability, and PerspectiveGap provides a foundation for measuring and improving it systematically.

</details>


### [104] [From Statute to Control Flow: Span-Grounded Deontic Trees for Defeasible Scope Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08932)
*Jian Chen,Siyuan Li,Chucheng Wan,Zixuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出NormBench基准（含2,290条法律条款，覆盖中英双语及跨语言场景），用于诊断规则遵循模型中的"静默范围遗漏"问题，并引入Span-Grounded Deontic Trees作为编译器式中间表示来显式建模条款的覆盖与例外关系。实验发现前沿LLM存在递归衰减和可审计性陷阱两种病理，使用SG-DT约束输出可提升整树保真度和例外恢复能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规则遵循模型在执行政策法规时，常常遗漏嵌套的例外或反例外条款，导致输出看似合规但在关键边缘案例上失败。现有法律NLP基准聚焦端任务结果，忽略了导致SSO的结构性遗漏问题，缺乏专门诊断和缓解此问题的基准与表示方法。

Method: 构建NormBench基准（2,290条条款，涵盖中国法律/地方政策、美国税法、GDPR、企业政策及跨语言场景），提出Span-Grounded Deontic Trees表示法，将每个逻辑分支锚定到原文跨度并要求显式排除守卫，实现确定性编译和审计。评估前沿LLM在该基准上的表现。

Result: 发现两种病理：递归衰减（表现随例外深度增加急剧下降）和可审计性陷阱（模型检索到相关跨度但未能组装正确控制流）。使用SG-DT作为约束中间输出可提升整树保真度和例外恢复，但其效用是机制特定的——增益集中在易发SSO的例外活跃案例上，当结构冗余或解析器保真度低时总体准确率可能混合。

Conclusion: SSO问题的根本瓶颈在于法规理解能力而非纯粹的智能体系统问题；SG-DT作为结构化中间表示能有效诊断和缓解SSO，但其增益具有条件性，需针对具体机制选择应用。

Abstract: Rule-following agents tasked with executing policies and regulations often fail via Silent Scope Omission (SSO): a model applies a general rule but silently drops nested exceptions or counter-exceptions, producing outputs that appear compliant yet break on important edge cases. Although such failures are often framed as an agentic-systems problem, the underlying bottleneck is statutory and policy understanding, a capability typically studied in legal NLP. However, most existing legal NLP benchmarks emphasize end-task outcomes, which can overlook the structural omissions that cause SSO. To diagnose and mitigate SSO, we introduce NormBench, a benchmark of 2,290 provisions spanning Chinese (laws and local policies), English (U.S. tax law, GDPR, and corporate policies), and cross-lingual settings, designed for defeasible scope parsing: identifying precisely which clause overrides which. NormBench uses Span-Grounded Deontic Trees (SG-DT), a compiler-style intermediate representation that anchors every logical branch to source spans and requires explicit exclusion guards, enabling deterministic compilation and audit. Evaluations of frontier LLMs reveal two recurring pathologies: (1) Recursion Decay, where performance drops sharply as defeater depth increases, and (2) an Auditability Trap, where models retrieve relevant spans but fail to assemble correct control flow. Using SG-DT as a constrained intermediate output improves whole-tree fidelity and defeater recovery, and downstream experiments show that its utility is mechanism-specific: gains concentrate on exception-active, SSO-prone cases, while aggregate accuracy can be mixed when the added structure is unnecessary or parser fidelity is low.

</details>


### [105] [PACT: Learning Diverse Diagnostic Strategies via Privileged Synthesis and Branch Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08938)
*Gen Li,Yuanze Hu,Zhichao Yang,Qingchen Yu,Jianwei Lv,Yue Guo,Yujing Liu,Faguo Wu,Hongwei Zheng,Xiandong Li,Bo Yuan,Yifan Sun,Zhaoxin Fan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出PACT框架，通过DPS数据合成多范式诊断对话，结合周期性符号共识聚合的Branch训练，在中文医疗诊断基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于LLM的医疗智能体虽有强医疗推理能力，但单范式或简单混合的对话监督方式使不同推理范式在训练中互相干扰，难以有效学习。

Method: 数据层面：DPS（医生-患者-监督者）利用完整电子病历进行质量控制，同时限制医生智能体只能访问患者可见信息，生成四种诊断推理范式下的验证对话。训练层面：PACT为每个范式训练专用的LoRA Branch，并通过符号共识（sign consensus）周期性聚合Branch到共享Anchor。

Result: 在构建的动态多轮中文医疗诊断基准上，PACT在诊断结果和咨询过程指标上均优于对比的通用、医疗专用和任务适配基线，达到SOTA。

Conclusion: 耦合多范式对话合成与共识Branch训练能够有效解决多推理范式学习中的干扰问题，提升临床诊断性能。

Abstract: Clinical diagnosis requires flexible use of multiple reasoning paradigms under incomplete patient information. Existing LLM-based medical agents show strong medical reasoning ability, but single-paradigm or naively mixed dialogue supervision makes these paradigms difficult to learn without interference. We propose \textbf{PACT} (Periodic Anchor Consensus Training), a framework that couples supervised multi-paradigm dialogue synthesis with consensus-based Branch training. At the data level, \textbf{DPS} (Doctor-Patient-Supervisor) uses complete electronic medical records (EMRs) for quality control while keeping the doctor agent restricted to patient-visible information. This produces validated dialogues under four diagnostic reasoning paradigms without leaking hidden clinical answers. At the training level, PACT trains one paradigm-specific LoRA Branch per paradigm and periodically aggregates Branches into a shared Anchor through sign consensus. We further construct a dynamic multi-turn Chinese medical diagnosis benchmark for interactive consultation. Experiments show that PACT achieves state-of-the-art performance among compared proprietary, medical-specialized, and task-adapted baselines on diagnostic outcome and consultation-process metrics.

</details>


### [106] [Multilingual Sentiment Aware Text Summarization A Reinforcement Learning Approach for Consistency Maintenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08940)
*Mikhail Krasitskii,Alexander Gelbukh,Olga Kolesnikova,Grigori Sidorov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了RLHF在文本摘要中导致的“情感漂移”现象——模型输出相比源文本系统地趋向中性情感。实验表明该现象普遍存在，且KL正则化是主要驱动因素。作者提出了一种情感感知的KL正则化改进方法，能在保持摘要质量的同时减轻情感漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF显著提升了文本摘要的质量和流畅性，但其对情感属性的影响尚未被充分理解。作者旨在探究RLHF对齐目标如何影响情感保留，并识别导致情感漂移的根本原因。

Method: 跨多个数据集、模型架构和八种语言进行广泛实验；提出Policy Attribution框架分解RLHF目标并量化各成分贡献；基于分析提出情感感知的KL正则化改进，选择性减少对情感承载标记的约束。

Result: 情感漂移是一致性现象，且随KL正则化强度增大而增强；KL正则化是情感抑制的主要驱动因素；提出的情感感知KL正则化能在保持摘要质量的同时减轻情感漂移。

Conclusion: 当前对齐方法在提升事实一致性和安全性的同时，可能无意中抑制了情感表达力。这提示需要发展明确考虑情感保留的对齐策略。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) has significantly improved the quality and fluency of large language models in text summarization. However, its impact on affective properties remains insufficiently understood. In this work, we study sentiment drift, a systematic shift toward neutral sentiment in RLHF-based summarization outputs compared to source texts. We conduct extensive experiments across multiple datasets, model architectures, and eight languages to analyze how alignment objectives influence sentiment preservation. Our results show that sentiment drift is a consistent phenomenon that becomes stronger with increased KL regularization strength, indicating a trade-off between alignment stability and affective fidelity. To explain this behavior, we introduce a Policy Attribution framework that decomposes the RLHF objective and quantifies the contribution of its components. Our analysis reveals that KL regularization is the primary driver of sentiment suppression across all settings. Based on these findings, we propose a sentiment-aware modification of the KL regularization term, which selectively reduces constraints on sentiment-bearing tokens. Empirical results demonstrate that this approach mitigates sentiment drift while maintaining summarization quality. Overall, our findings highlight a fundamental limitation of current alignment methods: while they improve factual consistency and safety, they may unintentionally suppress emotional expressiveness. This motivates the development of alignment strategies that explicitly account for affective preservation.

</details>


### [107] [CARE: A Conformal Safety Layer for Medical Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08969)
*Suhana Bedi,Bridget Lin,Anson Y. Zhou,Chloe O. Stanwyck,Jenelle A. Jindal,Sanmi Koyejo,David Stutz,Nigam H. Shah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CARE方法，一种基于共形风险控制的模型无关安全层，用于LLM医学摘要的错误检测，提供有限样本的分布自由保证，在控制遗漏和幻觉风险的同时减少审查负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在医学摘要中可能遗漏重要信息或引入无依据声明，现有错误检测方法缺乏对漏检错误的正式控制，无法在安全性和临床审查负担之间进行原则性权衡。

Method: 提出CARE（共形风险评估），通过两个控制器：幻觉控制器限制未标记幻觉句子的文档概率，遗漏控制器限制重要遗漏未被审查的期望比例。在完整(τ,γ)阈值空间上联合校准以保持正式保证，同时比替代校准基线减少最多5倍的标记句子。

Result: 在五个医学摘要任务上，CARE在α=0.15时满足目标风险界限（95%置信度），仅需约100个标注文档。初步临床研究（75份文档审查）中，校准标志将遗漏检测平均提高28.6个百分点。

Conclusion: 句子级别的安全保证在LLM辅助医学摘要是可行的，提供了调节残余风险和审查努力的可行机制。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for medical summarization, but their outputs can omit medically important information and introduce unsupported claims. Existing error-detection methods produce heuristic or uncalibrated scores, providing no formal control over missed errors and no principled way to trade off safety against clinician review burden. We introduce Conformal Assessment for Risk Evaluation (CARE), a post-hoc, model-agnostic safety layer that uses conformal risk control to overlay calibrated omission and hallucination flags onto summaries from any LLM without retraining. CARE provides finite-sample, distribution-free guarantees through two controllers: a hallucination controller that bounds the probability of a document containing any unflagged hallucinated sentence, and an omission controller that bounds the expected fraction of important omissions not surfaced for review. Unlike hallucination detection, omissions depend jointly on whether a source sentence is important and whether it is covered by the summary. We show that calibrating only one dimension can violate the target risk bound, while marginal decompositions remain valid but overly conservative. By jointly calibrating over the full $(τ,γ)$ threshold space, CARE preserves formal guarantees while surfacing up to 5$\times$ fewer sentences than alternative calibrated baselines. Across five medical summarization tasks, CARE satisfies the target risk bound at $α= 0.15$ with 95% confidence across 100 calibration/test resplits, using only ~100 labeled documents per domain. In a preliminary clinician study (75 document reviews), calibrated flags improved omission detection by 28.6 percentage points on average. These results show that sentence-level safety guarantees are feasible for LLM-assisted medical summarization and offer a tunable mechanism for balancing residual risk and review effort.

</details>


### [108] [Structure-Aware Modeling of Multiple-Choice Questions Improves Automatic Difficulty Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08988)
*Gabriel Ortega,Abelino Jiménez,Séverin Lions,Pablo Dartnell*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结构感知的自动试题难度估计方法，通过将多项选择题的干扰项作为独立文本输入进行显式建模，采用顺序感知拼接和顺序不变聚合两种架构，在智利数据集上（自然科学R²=0.83，社会科学R²=0.71）显著优于仅使用题干和正确答案的基线模型，并发现顺序不变变体在保持相近精度的同时参数量减半，证明了干扰项结构信息对难度预测的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前人关于干扰项信息能否一致提升试题难度预测效果的结论存在矛盾，本文假设干扰项的有效性取决于其结构表示方式，显式地将干扰项建模为独立组件应能比忽略该信息的基线模型更好地提升难度估计性能。

Method: 设计了受控架构，将MCQ组件作为不同输入进行建模：对每个干扰项独立编码，并通过顺序感知拼接（加位置标签）或顺序不变求和两种方式聚合表示。在智利2016-2020年自然科学和社会科学共4,114道选择题上评估，对比仅使用题干和正确答案的简单模型。

Result: 干扰项感知架构取得了更高预测性能，自然科学R²=0.83，社会科学R²=0.71；顺序不变变体在参数量减半的情况下达到几乎相同的精度，实现了精度与效率的良好权衡。

Conclusion: 结构信息（尤其是干扰项内容）是驱动预测精度提升的关键因素，支持开发面向大规模教育应用的计算高效的结构感知模型。

Abstract: Automatic Question Difficulty Estimation (AQDE) holds growing promise for educational assessment because it has the potential to yield difficulty estimates that are competitive with expert judgment, while helping reduce the time and financial burden associated with pilot administrations and scaling to digital testing contexts. Prior AQDE studies report mixed evidence on whether adding distractors as additional text to the question stem and the correct key consistently improves difficulty prediction. We hypothesize that the effectiveness of distractor information depends on its structural representation, and that explicitly modeling distractors as separate components improves difficulty estimation over baselines that omit this information. To address this, we designed controlled architectures that model MCQ components as distinct inputs to isolate the contribution of distractor content and order. Specifically, we represented distractors by encoding each distractor as its own text input and aggregating their representations either with order-aware concatenation (with positional tags) or with an order-invariant summation. We evaluated these architectures using two Chilean datasets (Natural and Social Sciences, 2016-2020; 4,114 multiple-choice questions). Compared to a simpler model that only used the question stem and the key, our best distractor-aware architecture achieved higher predictive performance, reaching R^2 = 0.83 for Natural Sciences and R^2 = 0.71 for Social Sciences items. An order-invariant variant achieved nearly the same accuracy with approximately half as many parameters, offering a favorable accuracy-efficiency trade-off. These results show that structural information (especially distractor content) drives gains in predictive accuracy, supporting the development of efficient, structure-aware models that are computationally viable for large-scale educational applications.

</details>


### [109] [Language-Aware Token Boosting: LLM Language Confusion Reduction Without Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08994)
*Trapoom Ukarapol,Pakhapoom Sarapat,Nut Chukamphaeng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种免微调的语言混淆缓解范式，通过LATB和Adaptive-LATB两种方法对目标语言相关token施加定向扰动，有效提升多语言对齐能力，同时保持摘要质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在生成非英语文本时经常出现语言混淆现象，现有方法依赖微调，成本高且不灵活。

Method: 提出免微调范式，包含两种方法：(1) LATB：对目标语言相关token施加定向扰动；(2) Adaptive-LATB：根据模型对目标语言的置信度动态调整扰动强度。

Result: 实验表明所提方法能有效减少语言混淆，提升多语言对齐能力，同时保持摘要生成质量，无需额外微调。

Conclusion: 免微调的语言混淆缓解方法是可行的，LATB和Adaptive-LATB为多语言生成提供了轻量级且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) sometimes exhibit language confusion when generating non-English text. Existing approaches typically rely on fine-tuning to mitigate this issue. In contrast, we propose a tuning-free paradigm for reducing language confusion. Within this paradigm, we introduce two methods: Language-Aware Token Boosting (LATB), which applies targeted perturbations to tokens associated with the desired language, and Adaptive Language-Aware Token Boosting (Adaptive-LATB), which dynamically adjusts these perturbations based on the model's confidence in the intended language. Experiments demonstrate that our methods effectively improve multilingual alignment by reducing language confusion, while maintain the summarization quality without requiring any additional fine-tuning. Our code is publicly available. https://github.com/scbdatax/genai-datax-language-aware-token-boosting.

</details>


### [110] [Beyond Averages: Evaluating LLMs on Human Survey Replication at the Distributional Level](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09013)
*Jeonghyeon Moon,Jiwon Kim,Yeheum Lah,Yoonju Han,Yuncheol Kang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLM能较好地复现人类调查的均值水平，但在分布层面（如购买数量的分布结构）表现不佳，均值评估可能具有误导性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究主要用均值或总体一致性评估LLM复现人类调查的能力，但忽略了能否复现人类行为的变异性（分布结构）。

Method: 使用2010年韩国方便面消费者选择实验的非公开数据，评估LLM在三种响应变量（二元购买行为、分类品牌选择、计数购买数量）上的均值、模式及分布对齐程度，并与仅基于人类数据的基线进行比较。

Result: LLM能合理复现条件级别的模式，但无法捕捉分布结构；在购买数量上，所有模型均未超过仅匹配人类合并分布的条件无关基线；均值匹配好的模型分布偏差可能更大，说明均值评估具有误导性；结构化角色和多模态输入提升复现效果，显式推理提示则持续降低效果。

Conclusion: 仅基于均值的评估无法全面反映LLM复现人类行为的能力，分布级对齐才是更严格的评估标准；输入配置对复现质量有显著影响。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly used to simulate human survey responses, but prior work has mainly evaluated replication using mean-level or aggregate agreement, offering limited insight into whether LLMs reproduce the variability of human behavior. We evaluate LLM-based survey replication at the distributional level using a non-public 2010 consumer choice experiment on Korean instant noodle purchases, a setting unlikely to overlap with model training data. We evaluate three response variables of differing statistical type: binary purchase incidence, categorical brand choice, and count purchase quantity. For each, we compare human and LLM responses at mean-level, pattern, and distributional alignment, and against reference baselines from the human data alone. LLMs reproduce condition-level patterns reasonably well but fail to capture distributional structure: for purchase quantity, no model beats a condition-insensitive baseline that simply matches the pooled human distribution. Because models that match human means well can still produce distributions further from humans than this baseline, mean-based evaluation alone can be actively misleading. Replication also varies with input configuration, with structured personas and multimodal inputs improving alignment while explicit reasoning prompting degrades it monotonically.

</details>


### [111] [SafeRun: Enabling Determinism in LLM Planning for Running](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09027)
*Meilin Chen,Zepeng Zhai,Jiaxuan Zhao,Yuan Lu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SafeRun提出了一种解耦架构，将LLM的柔性自然语言解释与确定性求解器的硬约束执行分离，在跑步规划中实现100%安全合规，同时保持自然语言灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在自然语言规划方面灵活性强，但在确定性关键领域（如跑步规划）因概率特性不可靠，违反安全规则会带来安全风险，现有方法无法同时保证安全性和灵活性。

Method: 提出解耦架构SafeRun，将LLM用于自然语言理解的软解释部分与确定性求解器用于硬约束强制执行的硬约束部分分离，实现严格安全约束与自然语言灵活性的兼顾。

Result: 在5种LLM上的实验表明，SafeRun达到100%安全分数（对比PE平均79.1%、CodeAct平均97.6%），同时保持有竞争力的指令跟随分数。

Conclusion: 解耦架构能有效解决LLM在确定性规划中的安全问题，为自然语言规划在安全关键领域的应用提供了可靠方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models enable flexible natural-language planning but remain unreliable in determinism-critical domains due to their probabilistic nature. This limitation is especially problematic in running planning, where violating safety rules can lead to safety risks. We propose SafeRun, a framework for deterministic LLM-based planning via a decoupled architecture. SafeRun separates soft interpretation by an LLM from hard constraint enforcement by a deterministic solver, ensuring strict safety constraints while preserving natural-language flexibility. To validate SafeRun, we build a comprehensive benchmark for running planning under realistic physiological and safety constraints. Experiments across five LLMs show that SafeRun achieves 100\% safety score (vs.\ 79.1\% PE average and 97.6\% CodeAct average) while maintaining competitive instruction-following scores. The SafeRun benchmark is publicly available at \href{https://huggingface.co/datasets/zzp-seeker/SafeRun-RunPlanning-Benchmark}{huggingface}.

</details>


### [112] [Bridging the Agent-World Gap: Text World Models for LLM-based Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09032)
*Yixia Li,Hongru Wang,Peng Lai,Zhiwen Ruan,He Zhu,Youxin Zhu,Ganlong Zhao,Minda Hu,Yun Chen,Sibei Yang,Peng Li,Jeff Z. Pan,Jia Pan,Guanhua Chen,Yang Liu,Guanbin Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 系统综述基于LLM智能体的文本世界模型（TWM），围绕形式化框架和智能体生命周期，涵盖基础定义、构建方法（LLM即世界模型/代码即世界模型）、应用（训练时经验合成/推理时规划验证）及评估等方面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的LLM智能体多为反应式，缺乏对交互式文本环境的结构与演化过程的显式建模，需要文本世界模型来支持规划、高效学习和原则性评估。

Method: 采用系统综述方法，围绕形式化框架和智能体生命周期组织文献，从基础定义、构建方法、应用场景和评估四个维度进行分类整理。

Result: 梳理了TWM的两大构建范式（LLM-as-WM和Code-as-WM），总结了TWM在训练时（经验合成）和推理时（规划、验证、适应）的支撑作用，并区分了对世界模型本身及其作为评估环境的评价方式。

Conclusion: 该综述整合了快速发展的文本世界模型领域，澄清了设计空间，并指出了未来研究的开放挑战。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based agents are increasingly used in interactive textual environments, from web navigation and code editing to tool use and long-horizon dialogue. Yet many remain largely reactive, mapping observations to actions without an explicit model of how these environments are structured and evolve. This motivates text world models (TWMs): transition models over textual states that, given a state and a candidate action, predict the resulting webpage, terminal output, API response, or user reply, thereby supporting planning, efficient learning, and principled evaluation. We systematically review text world models for LLM-based agents, organized around a formal framework and the agent lifecycle: (1) Foundations, defining text world models and characterizing them by state representation and grounding domain; (2) Construction, taxonomizing LLM-as-WM and code-as-WM paradigms and reviewing methods for building them; (3) Application, examining how world models support agents at training time through experience synthesis and at inference time through planning, verification, and adaptation; and (4) Evaluation, covering both evaluation of the world model itself and its use as an evaluation environment for agents. We aim to consolidate this rapidly developing area, clarify its design space, and highlight open challenges for future research.

</details>


### [113] [Emergent Misalignment Can Be Induced by Sycophancy and Reversed via Alignment Gating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09068)
*Sicheng Wang,Xiangyang Zhu,Han Wang,Zongrui Wang,Yuan Tian,Kaiwei Zhang,Kaiyuan Ji,Qi Jia,Guangtao Zhai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现谄媚微调（让模型被动同意用户错误观点）会引发广泛的紧急错配，并提出Alignment Gating方法——在微调中插入可学习的门控机制来识别并抑制导致不安全响应的内部表征，从而高效逆转紧急错配，且该方法具有良好的泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明在狭窄领域对恶意或错误输出进行微调会引发广泛的紧急错配，但高效逆转这种错配的方法仍然有限。

Method: 1) 识别谄媚微调作为紧急错配的新驱动因素；2) 提出Alignment Gating，在微调中插入可学习、可控的门控，通过门控学习识别负责不安全响应的内部表征，进而通过放大或抑制这些表征来加剧或缓解紧急错配。

Result: 谄媚微调会引发广泛且严重的错配行为；Alignment Gating模块表现出强泛化性，从窄领域微调获得的门控权重能显著抑制宽领域的错配行为，同时保持模型的一般能力。

Conclusion: Alignment Gating是一种高效且泛化能力强的紧急错配逆转方法，可用于修复因谄媚微调等行为导致的大语言模型对齐问题。

Abstract: Prior work has shown that fine-tuning large language models on malicious or incorrect outputs in narrow domains can induce broad misalignment and harmful behavior, a phenomenon known as emergent misalignment. However, efficient methods for reversing such misalignment remain limited. In this work, we make two contributions. First, we identify sycophancy fine-tuning, i.e., training models to passively agree with users' incorrect opinions, as a previously underexplored driver of emergent misalignment, and show that it induces broad and severe misaligned behavior. Second, we propose Alignment Gating, an efficient method for reversing emergent misalignment that inserts learnable and controllable gates into the model during fine-tuning. Through fine-tuning, these gates learn to identify the internal representations responsible for unsafe responses. Thus, amplifying or suppressing these representations then exacerbates or mitigates EM, respectively. We further find that alignment gating module exhibits strong generalization: gating weights obtained from narrow-domain fine-tuning substantially suppress broad-domain misaligned behavior while preserving the model's general capabilities.

</details>


### [114] [MAAM: Anchor-Preserving Compression and Contextual Calibration for Chinese Discriminatory Language Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09114)
*Yuxin Fu,Shijing Si*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MAAM（近视-散光锚定机制），一种受视觉模糊启发的轻量级模型无关框架，用于中文歧视性语言检测。通过保留歧视相关语义锚点并结合C-I-S上下文先验（语境基调、群体身份、立场极性）进行校准，在自建的中文LGBT歧视语言数据集ChLGBT（8,120条标注样本，含显性偏见、隐性偏见、情感强度三个序数标签）上，相比强编码器基线和前沿LLM基线均取得一致性提升，并在紧凑性和稳定性方面具有优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中文歧视性语言检测中，有害意图往往隐含且依赖上下文，现有方法难以有效捕捉；亟需一种轻量级、可解释且模型无关的检测方案，以替代对更大规模模型的依赖。

Method: 提出MAAM框架，其核心思想是模拟功能性视觉模糊：不保留所有token的同等重要性，而是保留歧视相关的语义锚点，并通过C-I-S上下文先验（Contextual Tone语境基调、Group Identity群体身份、Stance Polarity立场极性）对这些锚点进行校准。同时构建了首个中文LGBT歧视语言数据集ChLGBT，包含8,120条人工标注样本，标注维度为显性偏见、隐性偏见和情感强度三个序数标签。

Result: 在强编码器基线上，MAAM在显性偏见、隐性偏见和情感强度三个预测维度上均取得一致性提升，包括准确率、F1、Brier分数和期望校准误差。相比前沿LLM基线的零样本和少样本提示协议，MAAM在保持竞争力的同时展现出更强的紧凑性和稳定性。

Conclusion: 可解释的锚点保留与上下文校准方法为中文歧视性语言评估提供了一种实用的替代方案，无需依赖更大规模的模型扩展。

Abstract: Chinese discriminatory-language detection is challenging because harmful intent is often implicit and context-dependent. We propose MAAM (Myopia--Astigmatism Anchor Mechanism), a lightweight, model-agnostic framework inspired by functional visual blur: rather than preserving every token equally, MAAM retains discrimination-relevant semantic anchors and calibrates them with C--I--S contextual priors (Contextual Tone, Group Identity, and Stance Polarity). We also introduce ChLGBT, to our knowledge the first Chinese LGBT-focused discriminatory-language dataset, with 8,120 manually annotated samples and three ordinal labels: explicit bias, implicit bias, and emotional intensity. Across strong encoder baselines, MAAM improves all three prediction dimensions, with consistent gains in accuracy, F1, Brier score, and expected calibration error. Compared with frontier LLM baselines under zero-shot and few-shot prompting protocols, MAAM remains competitive while offering stronger compactness and stability. These results suggest that interpretable anchor preservation and contextual calibration provide a practical alternative to heavier model scaling for Chinese discriminatory-language assessment.

</details>


### [115] [Explicit Representation Alignment for Multimodal Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09148)
*Baode Wang,Ziming Wang,Huacan Wang,Ronghao Chen,Biao Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出利用视觉-语言模型将视觉内容转化为结构化文本描述，将异构模态投影到共享语言空间，并通过语义标记选择与批次级均匀性正则化相结合的混合学习策略，在多个多模态情感分析基准上取得最先进性能，揭示了表示对齐对多模态情感学习的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态情感分析中，多模态模型往往无法持续优于强文本基线，且不同融合策略的性能差异显著。现有方法缺乏对模态编码器表示对齐问题的关注，导致融合效果不稳定。

Method: 1) 利用视觉-语言模型将视觉内容转换为结构化文本描述，实现异构模态到共享语言空间的投影；2) 引入混合学习策略，结合语义标记选择与批次级均匀性正则化，分散并稳定全局特征空间，抑制VLM生成描述引入的噪声。

Result: 在多个多模态情感和情绪基准数据集上，该方法一致优于强单模态和多模态基线，达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: 表示对齐在多模态情感学习中比融合复杂度更为关键；本文提出的基于VLMs的文本中心推理框架有效提升了跨模态对齐与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Multimodal affective analysis aims to understand human sentiment and emotion by jointly modeling heterogeneous modalities such as text and images. However, multimodal models often fail to consistently outperform strong text-only baselines, with performance varying significantly across fusion strategies. In this work, we identify representation misalignment between independently pretrained modality encoders as a key bottleneck for effective multimodal learning, and show through controlled experiments that alignment prior to fusion is often more important than fusion complexity. To address this issue, we propose a unified multimodal affective analysis framework that leverages vision-language models (VLMs) to convert visual content into structured textual descriptions, projecting heterogeneous modalities into a shared linguistic space and enabling interpretable text-centric reasoning. To further improve robustness, we introduce a hybrid learning strategy that combines semantic token selection with a batch-level uniformity regularization objective, encouraging a more dispersed and stable global feature space while mitigating noise introduced by VLM-generated descriptions. Experiments on multiple multimodal sentiment and emotion benchmarks show that our method consistently outperforms strong unimodal and multimodal baselines, achieving state-of-the-art performance. Our analysis further highlights the critical role of representation alignment in multimodal affective learning.

</details>


### [116] [SEF-CLGC at SemEval-2026 Task 11: Logical Notation Impact on Language Model Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09157)
*Hanna Abi Akl,Fabien Gandon,Catherine Faron,Pierre Monnin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了SEF-CLGC框架，将形式逻辑符号与小语言模型(SLM)结合，用于评估SemEval-2026任务11子任务1中的推理性能。实验表明，仅依靠在自然语言和符号语言混合数据上训练的SLM，最佳模型在任务上达到27.80%的内容分数，同时显著降低了推理中的内容偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在结合形式逻辑符号与小语言模型，以降低大语言模型推理中的内容偏差，实现内容与形式推理的解耦评估。

Method: 采用SEF-CLGC框架，将形式逻辑符号与SLM结合，在自然语言和符号语言的混合数据上训练模型。

Result: 最佳模型在SemEval-2026 Subtask 1上获得27.80%的内容分数，并显著降低了推理中的内容偏差。

Conclusion: 仅依靠SLM结合形式逻辑符号训练，能够有效降低内容偏差，为解耦内容与形式推理提供了一种可行的技术路径。

Abstract: This paper revisits our pipeline called Syllogistic Evaluation Framework-Common Logic Grammar Construction (SEF-CLGC). We combine formal logical notations with Small Language Models (SLMs) to evaluate reasoning performance on the SemEval-2026 Task 11 Subtask 1: Disentangling Content and Formal Reasoning in Large Language Models. Our experiments show that by relying solely on SLMs, trained on a combination of natural and symbolic languages, our best model achieves a content score of 27.80% on the task while significantly lowering the content bias in reasoning.

</details>


### [117] [Unified Energy for Invariant and Independent Decoding in Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09159)
*Yuchen Yan,Minkai Xu,Zaiquan Yang,Yatao Bian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了扩散语言模型与自回归模型之间的性能差距，识别出三个关键因素（模型容量、依赖性和不变性），并提出统一能量函数（Uni-E）来修正分布偏移，从而提升并行文本生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型（DLMs）虽然支持并行文本生成，但与自回归（AR）基线之间存在性能差距，且随着并行度增加差距扩大。现有方法未能充分捕捉token间关系，亟需系统分析差距根源并提出有效解决方案。

Method: 首先系统分析性能差距的三个关键因素：模型容量（model capacity）、依赖性（dependency）和不变性（invariance）。针对不变性问题提出不变能量（Inv-E）及基于采样的估计器。进一步结合独立能量（Ind-E）得到统一能量（Uni-E），该能量无需基于采样的配分估计即可精确计算，且是模型无关的，可扩展到任意规模模型。

Result: 理论证明Uni-E能够修正由依赖性和不变性引起的分布偏移。在扩散语言模型（DLMs）和扩散大语言模型（DLLMs）上的大量实验验证了Uni-E的有效性。

Conclusion: Uni-E通过统一处理模型容量、依赖性和不变性三个关键因素，有效缩小了DLMs与AR模型之间的性能差距，且具有可精确计算、模型无关、可扩展等优势。

Abstract: Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) enable parallel text generation by iteratively denoising a full sequence, offering attractive flexibility compared to auto-regressive (AR) decoding. However, existing methods fail to fully capture token relationships, leading to a performance gap relative to AR baselines, especially as the degree of parallelism increases. In this paper, we give a systematic analysis of the gap, identifying three key factors: (i) model capacity, (ii) dependency, and (iii) invariance. To address these issues, we first propose an invariant energy (Inv-E) together with an effective sampling-based estimator to handle the invariance issue. By further combining with the independent energy (Ind-E), we obtain a unified energy (Uni-E), that accounts for all these factors. Uni-E enjoys a unique advantage: it can be computed exactly without sampling-based partition estimation. Besides, Uni-E is model agnostic and can therefore be scaled to models of arbitrary size. We further prove that Uni-E can correct the distribution shift caused by dependency and invariance. Extensive experiments across Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) and Diffusion Large Language Models (DLLMs) demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed Uni-E.

</details>


### [118] [Culturally-Adapted Red-Teaming Across East and Southeast Asian Contexts: A Methodological and Comparative Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09178)
*Hyeji Choi,Yongtaek Lim,Minwoo Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 直接翻译(DA)的英文安全基准无法反映目标语言的文化背景，导致对多语言大模型的安全评估存在系统性偏差。基于韩、日、泰、柬四种语言的配对数据集，研究者发现文化适配(CA)提示在所有16种语言×模型组合中均产生正向Delta-ASR（平均+9.3个百分点），且DT评估在48个类别×语言组合中有44个低估了风险。文化真实度方面，DT的文化深度(C3)得分均低于1.0/3.0（平均0.17），而CA得分最高达2.51，表明基于文化适配的基准测试对于有效的多语言LLM安全评估至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多语言大模型安全评估过度依赖对英文基准的直接翻译，这种方法仅转换表面语言形式，无法反映威胁场景、社会规范和法律框架中所嵌入的文化背景差异，可能导致安全评估结果失真。

Method: 通过1:1种子匹配方式，为韩语、日语、泰语和高棉语四种语言构建了成对的直接翻译(DT)和文化适配(CA)数据集，然后比较了四种开源LLM在攻击成功率(ASR)和文化真实度(Cultural Realism)两个维度上的表现差异。

Result: CA提示在所有16种语言×模型组合中Delta-ASR均大于0（平均+9.3pp）；DT评估在48个类别×语言组合中有44个低估了风险；DT的文化深度(C3)得分全部低于1.0/3.0（平均0.17），而CA得分最高达2.51；威胁形式分布在不同语言间呈现异质性。

Conclusion: 有效的多语言大模型安全评估不能仅依赖语言翻译，而必须将基准测试适配到语言特定的文化背景中，才能真实反映模型在实际多文化场景中的安全表现。

Abstract: Multilingual safety evaluation of large language models (LLMs) has predominantly relied on direct translation (DT) of English benchmarks into target languages - an approach that converts surface-level linguistic form while failing to reflect the cultural context embedded in threat scenarios, social norms, and legal frameworks. We construct paired DT and culturally-adapted (CA) datasets via 1:1 seed matching for four languages - Korean (KO), Japanese (JA), Thai (TH), and Khmer (KM) - and compare Attack Success Rate (ASR) and Cultural Realism scores across four open-source LLM. CA prompts yield Delta-ASR > 0 across all 16 language x model combinations (mean +9.3 pp), and DT-based evaluation underestimates risk in 44 of 48 category x language combinations. Language-level analysis reveals that the distribution of threat forms is heterogeneous across languages. Cultural Realism analysis further shows that DT Cultural Depth (C3) scores remain consistently below 1.0 out of 3.0 across all four languages (mean 0.17), whereas CA scores reach up to 2.51, indicating that direct translation produces inputs systematically divergent from those encountered in real-world multicultural settings. These findings demonstrate that adapting benchmarks to language-specific cultural contexts - rather than relying on linguistic translation alone - is necessary for valid multilingual LLM safety evaluation.

</details>


### [119] [Symbolic and Abstractive Reasoning with Complex Visual Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09195)
*Yichi Zhang,Jingdian Lu,Zhuo Chen,Lingbing Guo,Jun Xu,Wen Zhang,Huajun Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出复杂视觉查询（CVQ）这一新型抽象数据类型，用于探测多模态大语言模型的符号与抽象推理能力，并通过数据-范式-探索三维度系统研究，提出了基于知识图谱的CVQ合成管道和两阶段训练框架，实验验证了其在视觉推理及跨任务/场景泛化上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态大语言模型在处理抽象视觉内容的理解与推理方面仍存在局限，尤其是符号化和抽象化推理这一接近人类神经符号推理的关键能力尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出可扩展的CVQ合成管道，基于大规模多模态知识图谱，通过一阶逻辑算子的系统组合生成涵盖14种查询类型的多样化数据集；引入两阶段训练框架，逐步赋予MLLMs鲁棒的视觉推理能力。

Result: 在CVQ推理性能、跨任务和跨场景泛化等多个维度上进行了广泛实验，验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为推进MLLMs的推理前沿开辟了新的视角与途径。

Abstract: Understanding and reasoning over abstract visual content remains a challenge for current multi-modal large language models (MLLMs). In this paper, we explore a novel abstract data type termed complex visual query (CVQ), designed to probe symbolic and abstractive reasoning, which is a critical yet underexplored dimension of human-like neuro-symbolic reasoning for MLLMs. We present a comprehensive investigation from three perspectives: \textbf{Data $\times$ Paradigm $\times$ Exploration}. Specifically, we propose a scalable pipeline for synthesizing CVQs grounded in large-scale multi-modal knowledge graphs, generating a diverse dataset encompassing 14 distinct query types via systematic combinations of first-order logic operators. We further introduce a two-stage training framework that progressively equips MLLMs with robust visual reasoning capabilities. We conduct extensive experiments to rigorously evaluate MLLMs across multiple dimensions, including reasoning performance on CVQs, as well as cross-task and cross-scenario generalization. We believe our work opens new perspectives and avenues for advancing the reasoning frontiers of MLLMs.

</details>


### [120] [TruthSplit: Operationalizing Conditional Validity in Arguments Through Multi-Perspective Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09251)
*Benjamin Stieger,Maximilian Terberger,Thomas Huber,Christina Niklaus*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TruthSplit是一个交互式系统，通过条件有效性分析，从多视角对论证进行评估，揭示不同世界观下同一主张可能得出不同结论的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有论证分析工具通常只分析论证本身的结构、质量、立场或说服力等属性，而忽略了视角特定的背景知识。TruthSplit旨在弥补这一空白，支持探索性分析，揭示同一主张在不同世界观（价值观、假设、概念定义）下如何导致不同结论。

Method: 给定输入论证文本，系统提取主张和前提，采用三层自然语言推理(NLI)方法评估逻辑一致性和世界观特定的规范性一致性，并将LLM推理条件化为结构化世界观档案（编码核心价值和决策原则），生成视角特定解释，识别价值冲突和假设差距，通过交互式分析界面可视化分歧。

Result: 实现了多视角论证分析系统，能够进行条件有效性分析，识别价值冲突和假设差距，并通过交互界面可视化论证分歧。

Conclusion: TruthSplit通过显式建模世界观-特定的价值观和假设，填补了论证分析中视角背景知识缺失的关键空白，为理解论证分歧提供了新的交互式分析范式。

Abstract: We present TruthSplit, an interactive system for multi-perspective argument analysis. Existing argumentation tools typically analyze properties of the argument itself, such as structure, quality, stance, or persuasiveness, while leaving perspective-specific background knowledge implicit. TruthSplit addresses this gap by supporting an exploratory analysis of how the same claim can lead to different conclusions when interpreted through worldview-specific values, assumptions, and conceptual definitions. We refer to this perspective-dependent analysis as conditional validity. Given an input argumentative text, TruthSplit extracts claims and premises, applies a three-layer natural language inference (NLI) approach to assess both logical and worldview-specific normative consistency, and conditions large language model (LLM) reasoning on structured worldview profiles that encode core values and decision principles. The system then generates perspective-specific interpretations, identifies value conflicts and assumption gaps, and visualizes divergence through interactive analytical interfaces.

</details>


### [121] [One Model, Multiple Goals: Adaptive Multi-Objective Learning for E-commerce Dialogue Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09293)
*Mingzhe Li,Jing Xiang,Enguo Zhou,Lang Gao,Tai Li,Qishen Zhang,Xiangliang Zhang,Xiuying Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出MORE框架，一种自适应多目标强化学习方法，通过将推理函数作为约束条件、引入自适应多奖励机制，在电商对话系统中联合优化推理准确性和语言自然度，在字节跳动和MultiWOZ 2.2上取得显著提升，在线转化率提升30.09%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商对话系统需同时满足推理准确性（如用户资格、信用额度判断）和语言自然度两个目标，但两者优化动力学不同，直接混合奖励会导致振荡和不稳定学习。

Method: 1) 将推理函数作为约束条件指导策略优化，而非优化单一混合奖励；2) 推理时直接生成回复，无需显式推理步骤，避免额外推理开销；3) 引入自适应多奖励机制，通过梯度反馈动态重加权流畅度、自然度等语言信号。

Result: 在字节跳动14天在线实验中，整体转化率提升16.53%，达成转化率提升30.09%；用户满意度提升，转接率降低；人机对比中恢复约60%的人类客服转化提升。

Conclusion: MORE通过将推理作为约束而非奖励、动态平衡多目标的方式，有效解决了电商对话系统中推理与生成目标的冲突问题，在真实生产环境中验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Dialogue systems in e-commerce scenarios often need to satisfy multiple objectives: accurately reasoning over user profiles (e.g., eligibility, credit limit) to ensure correct decision-making and user state interpretation, while also generating natural and faithful responses. These goals are complementary but not identical. In this work, we propose MORE, an adaptive Multi-Objective REinforcement learning framework that jointly optimizes reasoning accuracy and linguistic naturalness. Our preliminary experiments show that directly mixing rewards with diverging optimization dynamics can cause oscillations and unstable learning. Thus, instead of optimizing a single mixed reward, we treat reasoning functions as constraints that guide policy optimization. At inference time, the system directly generates responses without explicit reasoning steps, while still benefiting from reasoning-enhanced scaffold and avoiding additional inference overhead. To better balance linguistic objectives during response generation, we introduce an adaptive multi-reward mechanism that aggregates signals such as fluency and naturalness and dynamically reweighs them via gradient feedback. We evaluate MORE on two real-world dialogue systems at ByteDance and the MultiWOZ 2.2 benchmark, where it consistently outperforms strong baselines. In 14-day online experiments on ByteDance production traffic, MORE improves overall and reached conversion by 16.53% and 30.09%, while increasing user satisfaction and reducing handoff rates. Notably, in a human-machine comparison, MORE recovers about 60% of the incremental conversion lift achieved by human agents.

</details>


### [122] [NüshuVoice: Reviving the Voice of Endangered Nüshu with Pitch-Aware Text-to-Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09295)
*Hongkun Yang,Xinhui Yi,Xiyan Zhao,Yibo Meng,Lionel Z. Wang,Lixu Wang,Yaqi Zhang,Ruiqi Chen,Xuanyue Zhao,Lanxin Zhang,Yu Zeng,Weijia Chu,Yiming Ma,Chenyu Liu,Jianghao Lin,Xin Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个女书文本转语音基准NüshuVoice，并设计了基于F0条件的VITS框架Nüshu-PitchVITS，利用五级音高标注作为韵律归纳偏置，在极低资源条件下实现了女书的语音合成，效果优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 女书是一种濒危的 phonetic 文字，现有的计算研究主要集中于文本数字化和视觉识别，而对其真实发音的声学重建几乎未被探索；构建女书TTS系统面临录音数据极度稀缺且多为孤立音节而非自然语句的挑战。

Method: 构建了句子级女书文本-音频数据集，对齐了标准化Unicode女书文本、音标、中文翻译和档案录音；提出Nüshu-PitchVITS，一种F0条件化的VITS框架，将女书的五级音高标注作为显式的韵律归纳偏置。

Result: Nüshu-PitchVITS在频谱保真度、音高重建和人工评价的可懂度上均优于强TTS基线。

Conclusion: 本文首次为女书建立了TTS基准，证明了在极端低资源条件下利用音高标注作为偏置可以有效合成濒危语言的语音，数据集和代码已公开。

Abstract: Nüshu is an endangered phonetic script historically used by women in Jiangyong County, southern Hunan, China. While existing computational studies of Nüshu mainly focus on textual digitization and visual recognition, the acoustic reconstruction of its authentic pronunciation remains largely unexplored. Building a Nüshu text-to-speech (TTS) system is particularly challenging because available recordings are extremely limited and mostly consist of isolated syllable-level pronunciations rather than natural sentence-level utterances. In this work, we introduce NüshuVoice, the first TTS benchmark for Nüshu. We construct a sentence-level Nüshu text-to-audio dataset that aligns standardized Unicode Nüshu text, phonetic transcriptions, standard Chinese translations, and archival recordings. To synthesize speech under this extreme low-resource setting, we propose Nüshu-PitchVITS, an F0-conditioned VITS framework that leverages Nüshu's five-level pitch notation as an explicit prosodic inductive bias. Experimental results show that Nüshu-PitchVITS outperforms strong TTS baselines in spectral fidelity, pitch reconstruction, and human-rated intelligibility. We publicly release the dataset and code at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Nvshu-TTS-2EB6.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [123] [PathoSage: Towards Multi-Source Evidence Adjudication in Pathology via Experience-Aware Agentic Workflow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07549)
*Chengyang Zhang,Wenchuan Zhang,Bo Li,Mengran Li,Bob Zhang,Yuhao Yi,Hong Bu,Jiancheng Lv*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PathoSage是一种三阶段框架，通过显式分离知识检索、证据收集和证据裁决，并结合结构化证据审议和Beta-Bernoulli经验系统，有效缓解病理学多模态推理中的幻觉和分类器分歧问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的端到端病理MLLM容易产生形态学特征幻觉，而基于智能体的系统将工具输出和检索知识合并到共享上下文中，导致决策易受冲突证据和上下文污染影响。

Method: 提出PathoSage三阶段框架：(1) 显式分离知识检索、证据收集和证据裁决；(2) 核心组件结构化证据审议独立评估异构证据、进行冲突分析并在全新上下文中生成最终判断以减少锚定偏差；(3) 引入无需训练的Beta-Bernoulli经验系统，通过连续信用分配建模长期工具可靠性并构建相似性加权先验。

Result: PathoSage有效缓解了VQA幻觉和分类器分歧问题，在病理MLLM和智能体基线上表现更优。

Conclusion: 显式的证据裁决和可靠性感知的工具建模是构建鲁棒病理智能体的关键要素。

Abstract: Recent advances in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) and agent workflows have shown strong promise for computational pathology, yet reliable patch-level reasoning remains challenging. End-to-end pathology MLLMs often hallucinate morphological features, while recent agentic systems usually merge tool outputs and retrieved knowledge into a shared context, making decisions vulnerable to conflicting evidence and context contamination. We propose PathoSage, a three-stage framework that explicitly separates knowledge retrieval, evidence collection, and evidence adjudication for patch-level pathology multimodal reasoning. Its core component, Structured Evidence Deliberation, independently evaluates heterogeneous evidence from tools, performs conflict analysis, and generates the final judgment in a fresh context to reduce anchoring bias. We further introduce a training-free Beta-Bernoulli experience system with continuous credit assignment to model long-term tool reliability and construct similarity-weighted priors for future tool use. Experiments show that PathoSage effectively mitigates VQA hallucinations and classifier disagreement, outperforming strong pathology MLLM and agentic baselines. Our results highlight explicit evidence adjudication and reliability-aware tool modeling as key ingredients for robust pathology agents.

</details>


### [124] [OmniMem: Perturbation-aware Memory Compression for Streaming Audio-Visual LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07577)
*Guangzhi Sun,Yixuan Li,Yudong Yang,Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OmniMem是一种针对音视频大语言模型设计的流式推理框架，通过模态感知的内存分配、扰动感知的KV状态选择以及预算感知的微调，在相同内存预算下比训练无关压缩方法提升2-4%准确率，微调后再提升1-2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音视频LLM在处理长视频时，视频token和KV缓存呈线性增长，而现有压缩方法对所有token一视同仁，忽视了视觉与音频模态间严重的token不平衡问题，导致长视频推理效率低下。

Method: 1) 模态感知内存分配策略：分别管理视觉和音频上下文，解决模态间token不平衡；2) 扰动感知内存选择：保留信息丰富且非冗余的KV状态；3) 预算感知微调：在部署约束下促使模型将有用信息整合到保留内存中。

Result: 在VideoMME Long、LVBench和LVOmniBench上使用video-SALMONN 2+和Qwen-2.5-Omni进行评估，相同内存预算下OmniMem比训练无关压缩基线提升2-4%绝对准确率，微调后额外提升1-2%。

Conclusion: OmniMem通过模态感知和扰动感知的设计有效解决了音视频LLM长视频推理中的内存效率问题，验证了面向多模态场景的差异化内存管理策略的有效性。

Abstract: Audio-visual large language models (LLMs) hold strong promise for long-form video understanding, yet their long-video inference is fundamentally limited by the linear growth of video tokens and key-value (KV) caches. We present OmniMem, a memory-efficient streaming framework designed specifically for audio-visual LLMs. Unlike existing compression methods that treat all tokens uniformly, OmniMem introduces a modality-aware memory allocation strategy that separately manages visual and audio contexts, addressing the severe token imbalance between the two modalities. OmniMem further preserves informative and non-redundant KV states through perturbation-aware memory selection, enabling compact memory without sacrificing long-range understanding. To strengthen compression under realistic deployment constraints, we also explore budget-aware fine-tuning, which encourages the model to consolidate useful information into retained memory. Experiments on VideoMME Long, LVBench, and LVOmniBench with video-SALMONN 2+ and Qwen-2.5-Omni show that OmniMem consistently improves over strong training-free compression baselines by 2-4% absolute accuracy under the same memory budgets, with an additional 1-2% gain after fine-tuning.

</details>


### [125] [Syll: Open-Source Personal Automation with Cross-Surface Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07594)
*Bo Zhang,Borui Zhang,Chenghao Jiang,Minglei Shi,Xiaofeng Wang,Zheng Zhu,Jie Zhou,Jiwen Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Syll 是一个开源、自托管的 multimodal agent 框架，统一了 MCP/API 工具、CLI 执行和 GUI 控制，支持用户通过演示教学技能，并将执行过程转化为多模态证据供审计，已在 Adobe Photoshop、Audition、Stardew Valley、macOS Finder 等桌面应用中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个人 AI 代理大多局限于单一界面（如仅 API 或仅 GUI），缺乏对用户教学和可审计性的支持，难以在异构接口间协调操作。

Method: Syll 构建了模块化运行时，统一 MCP/API 工具、CLI 和 GUI 控制；核心是双向用户-代理交互层：用户通过直接演示教学，Syll 编译为可复用技能；代理执行反向转化为日志、关键帧和审批检查点多模态证据；外部化记忆、技能、例程和治理为可编辑本地工件。

Result: 在 Adobe Photoshop、Audition、Stardew Valley、macOS Finder 等生产桌面应用上验证；开展了面向机制的实验，验证了多模态路由、可教学 GUI 回放和持久化本地工件。

Conclusion: Syll 可作为个人自动化的实用开源基础，支持用户教学、审计和持续扩展。

Abstract: Personal AI agents must increasingly operate across APIs, shells, web surfaces, and desktop GUIs, yet many systems remain tuned to a single interface and offer limited support for user teaching and auditability. We present Syll, an open-source, self-hosted multimodal agent harness that unifies MCP/API tools, CLI execution, and visual GUI control in a modular runtime, enabling agents to coordinate computer use across heterogeneous interfaces while streamlining how users and agents exchange information. At the core of Syll is a bidirectional user-agent interaction layer: users teach procedures through direct demonstration, which Syll compiles into reusable skills; agent execution is translated back into multimodal evidence -- logs, keyframes, and approval checkpoints -- for inspection and control. Syll further externalizes memory, skills, routines, and governance as editable local artifacts, supporting straightforward inspection, extension, and downstream development. Our implementation has been validated on production desktop applications including Adobe Photoshop, Adobe Audition, Stardew Valley, macOS Finder and others. We report mechanism-oriented studies that validate multimodal routing, teachable GUI replay, and persistent local artifacts. We hope Syll can serve as a practical open-source foundation for personal automation that users can teach, inspect, and continuously extend.

</details>


### [126] [A case study of evaluating AI agents on a neuroscience data-to-discovery pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07718)
*Kai A. Horstmann,Ethan Lin,Alice A. Robie,Jennifer J. Sun,Kristin Branson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文实证研究了通用编码智能体在果蝇光遗传学数据到发现的完整流水线上的表现。智能体能解决部分流水线阶段，但在需要科学判断而非预定义标准时表现挣扎，端到端完整求解超出其当前能力。研究识别了计算资源管理、大规模数据泛化等新挑战，并提炼了构建科学任务与评估标准的原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科研管线中某些阶段需要领域专家花费数天到数月完成，科学家更关注正确性和鲁棒性而非实现细节，Agentic AI工具为自动化这些软件开发瓶颈提供了有前景的路径。

Method: 在果蝇光遗传学数据到发现的完整流水线上对通用编码智能体进行实证研究，使用比现有基准大得多的数据集和基于领域专家标准的评估准则，并分析智能体的代码迭代过程。

Result: 智能体可以解决若干独立流水线阶段，表明阶段级自动化可行；但当缺乏预定义迭代标准而需依赖科学判断评估自身解决方案时表现最差；视觉自评估大多失败；端到端完整求解超出当前能力。

Conclusion: 计算资源管理和大规模数据泛化等挑战在现有基准中很少被涉及；为开放性问题构建科学任务和严格评估标准需要提炼新的原则。

Abstract: Agentic AI tools offer a promising path to automating software development bottlenecks in scientific research pipelines, particularly for stages that take domain experts days to months to build, where scientists care about correctness and robustness, not implementation details. We present an empirical study of general-purpose coding agents on a fly optogenetics data-to-discovery pipeline. We assess agents on tasks substantially larger than existing benchmarks, datasets orders of magnitude bigger, and evaluation criteria grounded in domain expert standards. We show that agents can solve several individual pipeline stages, suggesting stage-level automation is tractable. By analyzing agents' code iterations, we show that they struggle most when there is not a pre-defined criterion to iterate on, and they must instead use their scientific judgment to assess their current solution, a key open challenge. Mirroring scientific practice, they sometimes attempt visual inspection of intermediate outputs for self-evaluation, but largely fail to interpret what they see or act on it appropriately. Solving the end-to-end pipeline correctly requires stringing together successes across all pipeline stages, and this is beyond agents' current abilities. We identify challenges largely absent from existing benchmarks, including computational resource management and generalization to large held-out data collections. Finally, we distill principles for constructing scientific tasks and rigorous evaluation criteria for open-ended problems.

</details>


### [127] [Why Limit the Residual Stream to Layers and Not Tokens? Persistent Memory for Continuous Latent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07720)
*Mujtaba Farhan,Maheep Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出AGCLR方法，通过门控概念流（写门、读门、忘门）解决CoCoNuT在潜在空间推理时的概念瓶颈问题，即在连续推理过程中中间隐状态被覆盖导致信息丢失。实验表明AGCLR在GSM8K、HotpotQA和ProsQA上均优于CoCoNuT和CoT基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoCoNuT虽然在潜在空间中同时探索多条推理路径，但存在概念瓶颈：每次推理传递时中间隐状态被覆盖，导致模型随着推理深度增加而丢失早期计算的关键事实，在HotpotQA上甚至不如CoT基线。

Method: 提出AGCLR（自适应门控连续潜在推理），在CoCoNuT基础上增加持久残差记忆（Gated Concept Stream），通过三个门控机制控制：写门将中间事实提交到记忆、读门检索相关先验状态、忘门修剪无关上下文。

Result: 在GSM8K、HotpotQA和ProsQA上使用GPT-2作为基模型，AGCLR在所有数据集上均取得一致性提升，且随着课程深度增加性能差距进一步扩大，直接解决了概念瓶颈问题。CoCoNuT在HotpotQA上EM仅10.4%，低于CoT的11.0%。

Conclusion: AGCLR通过门控概念流有效缓解了潜在空间推理中的信息遗忘问题，在多种推理任务上均优于现有方法，证实了持久化中间推理状态对于深度推理的重要性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable reasoning abilities on mathematical and multi-hop planning tasks. The CoCoNuT (Chain of Continuous Thought) paradigm~\cite{hao2024coconut} extends this by enabling models to reason in latent space, exploring multiple reasoning paths simultaneously rather than committing to a single chain early on. However, we identify a limitation we term the \textbf{concept bottleneck}. At each reasoning pass, intermediate hidden states are overwritten, causing the model to lose critical facts computed in earlier steps as reasoning depth increases. We observe this empirically. On HotpotQA, vanilla CoCoNuT (10.4\% EM) fails to improve over the CoT baseline (11.0\% EM), and performance degrades with curriculum depth on GSM8K. To address this, we propose \textbf{AGCLR} (Adaptive Gated Continuous Latent Reasoning), which augments CoCoNuT with a \textit{Gated Concept Stream}. A persistent residual memory maintained across all reasoning passes, controlled by three learned gates: a \textit{write} gate that commits intermediate facts to memory, a \textit{read} gate that retrieves relevant prior states, and a \textit{forget} gate that prunes irrelevant context. Evaluated on GSM8K, HotpotQA, and ProsQA using GPT-2 as our base model, AGCLR achieves consistent improvements across all types of datasets. With the performance gap compounding as curriculum depth increases, directly resolving the concept bottleneck. Code available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/JJJJ/README.md

</details>


### [128] [Automatic Extraction of Structured Information from Brain MRI Reports Using an Open-Weight Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07721)
*Kaouther Mouheb,Amos Pomp,Antoine Manenti,Romy de Haan,Farog Faghir,Joy Martens,Harro Seelaar,Francesco Mattace-Raso,Meike W. Vernooij,Frank J. Wolters,Stefan Klein,Esther E. Bron*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了LLaMA 3.1在荷兰语神经放射学报告中自动提取数据的能力。研究发现，该模型在视觉评分、病灶检测等任务上表现优异（准确率82%-96%），但数值变量（如微出血数量）表现较低（66%-80%）。通过少样本提示和结构相似性选择策略，数值变量性能提升至81%-92%。英文翻译提示与荷兰语直接提示效果相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从自由文本放射学报告中自动提取数据可支持大规模研究，但目前少有研究评估大语言模型在荷兰语神经放射学报告上的表现。

Method: 分析947份脑部MRI报告（来自记忆门诊，2016-2021年），由受训医学生标注30个变量。评估LLaMA 3.1在不同语言（荷兰语vs英译）和少样本提示（不同示例选择策略）下的性能，使用平衡准确率、准确率、平均绝对误差和文本相似度等指标，在10次随机划分上计算。

Result: LLaMA 3.1零样本表现：内侧颞叶萎缩（左90%、右96%）、全脑皮质萎缩（87%）、Fazekas评分（94%）、微出血检出（93%）、梗死检出（82%）、病灶位置文本相似度（0.95）。数值变量表现较低：微出血数量（80%）、梗死数量（66%）。英译效果相当。少样本提示将微出血数值提升至92%、梗死数值提升至81%。

Conclusion: LLaMA 3.1在荷兰语神经放射学报告数据提取中展现强大潜力，少样本提示可提升数值变量性能，但位置相关变量仍存在挑战。

Abstract: Objectives: Automatic data extraction from free-text radiology reports enables large-scale research, but few studies assessed the performance of large language models (LLMs) on Dutch neuroradiology reports. Methods: We analyzed 947 brain MRI reports from a tertiary memory clinic (2016-2021), authored by consultant neuroradiologists. Trained medical students annotated thirty variables; 100 reports were double-annotated to assess inter-rater reliability. We evaluated the performance of the open-weight LLM LLaMA 3.1 using different languages (Dutch vs. English translation) and few-shot prompting with different example selection strategies. Performance was evaluated using balanced accuracy for categorical variables, accuracy and mean absolute error for counts, and text similarity for free-text. Metrics were computed across 10 random splits of the 947 reports. Results: LLaMA 3.1 demonstrated high zero-shot performance for visual rating scores (mean [95%-CI]): Medial Temporal Atrophy: 90% [77-100%] on the left and 96% [94-99%] on the right, Global Cortical Atrophy: 87% [83-91%], and Fazekas: 94% [93-96%]. Microbleed mentions were detected with 93% accuracy [92-95%] and infarct mentions with 82% [80-84%]. Text similarity for lesion location reached 0.95 [0.95-0.96]. Performance was lower for numerical variables: 80% [78-82%] for the number of microbleeds and 66% [63-68%] for infarcts. English translation yielded comparable results. Few-shot prompting improved performance for numerical variables, achieving 92% [90-93%] for microbleeds and 81% [77-85%] for infarcts using structural similarity-based selection. Conclusion: LLaMA 3.1 shows strong potential for extracting data from Dutch neuroradiology reports. Few-shot prompting enhances performance for numerical variables, whereas challenges remain for location-specific variables.

</details>


### [129] [Some hypotheses on how chatbots work in problem-solving-driven conversations. Large Language Models as confirmation of the Innovation Illusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07722)
*S. F. M. van Vlijmen,H. D. Lethe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文基于聚合动力学、认知语言学、神经心理学和心理学，论证基础聊天机器人（由LLM+简单界面构成）无法成为真正的人类思维伙伴，原因在于LLM训练数据仅部分模仿人类思维，编码的是人工隐喻问题传播而非真实理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在从跨学科视角澄清聊天机器人的能力边界，回应Big Tech的乐观主义，为理解聊天机器人的功能、益处和缺陷提供社会和政治层面重要的理论贡献。

Method: 整合Aggregation Dynamics、认知语言学、神经心理学和心理学四大学科理论，聚焦基础聊天机器人（LLM+简单界面），通过分析人类理解与思维的"隐喻问题传播"机制，对比LLM训练数据的特性及编码过程，进行理论推演。

Result: 提出了人类思维基于隐喻问题传播的描述；假设LLM训练文本数据集具有特定特征且仅部分模仿人类思维；假设训练过程将人工隐喻问题传播编码进LLM；结论是基础聊天机器人无法成为与人类匹配的思维伙伴，且LLM进一步发展也无法实现这一点。

Conclusion: 聊天机器人无法成为真正的人类思维伙伴，与Yann LeCun的观点一致，与Big Tech的乐观主义相悖；尽管存在局限性，但聊天机器人已被大规模使用，因此理解其本质具有重要的社会政治意义。

Abstract: This article offers a perspective on the nature of chatbots as genuine conversation partners when discussing problems in relation to their solutions. What can chatbots do and what can't they do, and how can this be explained? Our argument draws on Aggregation Dynamics, Cognitive Linguistics, Neuropsychology and Psychology.
  Our argument focuses on basic chatbots in the hope of thereby making statements about the core functionality of more advanced chatbots. Basic chatbots are assumed to consist of a Large Language Model (LLM) with a simple interface.
  The main results are: a description of human understanding and thinking based on so-called metaphorical problem propagations; the hypothesis that text dataset used for training LLMs have specific characteristics and that these text datasets only partially imitate human thinking and understanding; the hypothesis that the LLM training process encodes artificial metaphorical problem propagations into an LLM from these datasets; our conclusion that a basic chatbot cannot be a thinking partner capable of matching humans; our conclusion that further development of the Large Language Model will not lead to this either.
  Yann LeCun states: "Animals and humans exhibit learning abilities and understandings of the world that are far beyond the capabilities of current AI and machine learning (ML) systems." Our conclusions are in line with this. LeCun's vision and ours are at odds with the optimism of Big Tech. That does not alter the fact that chatbots exist, that they are being used on a massive scale, by both individuals and organisations, and that it is therefore socially and politically important to understand them. Our article aims to contribute to the discussion on the functioning, benefits and drawbacks of chatbots.
  We have not yet encountered the approach we used to arrive at our conclusions in our research into how chatbots work.

</details>


### [130] [Land cover and flood type govern the detection limits of satellite-based flood mapping across diverse global flood events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07780)
*Venkatesh Kolluru,Rajat Shinde,Abdelhak Marouane,Caden Helbling,Deepak Shah,Othneil Drew,Iksha Gurung,Manil Maskey,Rahul Ramachandran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文利用地理空间基础模型Prithvi-EO-2.0对19次全球洪水事件（2017-2025）进行淹没制图，发现检测精度取决于土地覆盖和洪水类型，耕地检测最高（IoU=52%），树木和建成区几乎无法检测（IoU=4%），并识别出23种失败模式，其中流程工程问题占主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 洪水灾害频发且气候变暖加剧，卫星遥感制图对灾害响应至关重要，但现有地理空间基础模型在未见过的多样化洪水事件中的操作可靠性尚未被充分表征。

Method: 部署Prithvi-EO-2.0模型，在覆盖六大洲、八个气候带和六种洪水机制的19次分布外洪水事件上进行测试，使用两个独立参考产品进行验证，并通过迭代流程测试识别失败模式。

Result: 耕地检测一致性最高（IoU=52%），河流型洪水检测最强（F1=0.69）；树木覆盖和建成区检测极低（IoU=4%）；双参考验证显示部分误差源于参考产品间的定义不一致；识别出23种失败模式，流程工程误差远大于模型能力限制。

Conclusion: 研究建立了面向操作化卫星洪水制图的环境依赖型检测边界，揭示了土地覆盖和洪水类型对检测精度的决定性影响，强调了流程工程在模型部署中的关键作用。

Abstract: Floods are among the most destructive natural hazards, and their increasing frequency under climate change makes satellite-based inundation mapping essential for disaster response. Geospatial foundation models pretrained on satellite archives offer geographic transferability, but their operational reliability across diverse, unseen events remains uncharacterized. Here we deploy Prithvi-EO-2.0 across 19 out-of-distribution flood events (2017-2025) spanning six continents, eight climate zones, and six flood mechanisms, validating against two independent reference products. Detection accuracy depended jointly on land cover and flood type, with cropland yielding the highest agreement (IoU=52%) and riverine events the strongest detection (F1=0.69), while tree cover and built-up areas showed near-zero detection (IoU=4%) regardless of flood mechanism. Dual-reference validation revealed that apparent model error partly reflects definitional inconsistency between reference products rather than detection failure. Iterative pipeline testing identified 23 failure modes, with pipeline engineering dominating initial error over model capacity. These findings establish environment-dependent detection boundaries for operational satellite flood mapping.

</details>


### [131] [Reconstructing and forecasting disease trajectories of patients with Alzheimer's disease using routine data in resource-constrained settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07798)
*Ratnadeep Das,Atri Chatterjee,Sitikantha Roy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GNOVA框架（GRU-神经ODE变分自编码器），利用常规就诊数据（无需MRI、PET等昂贵模态）实现认知评分的双向预测（插值与外推）及不确定性量化，在ADNI数据集上取得良好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作主要聚焦预测未来认知状态，较少关注从过去就诊记录重建完整轨迹；量化预测不确定性的研究不足；且依赖MRI、PET、CSF等昂贵模态，限制了在资源有限场景的部署。

Method: 提出GNOVA统一框架：GRU编码器支持任意数量、任意时间点的输入；神经ODE解码器实现连续估计，支持任意时间点的插值与外推；变分自编码器框架提供不确定性估计。

Result: 基于ADNI数据集1,727名患者、10年数据，CDR-SB和MMSE的MAE分别为1.35和2.28，无需神经影像或生物标志物数据；消融实验显示年龄、BMI和APOE4基因为强预测因子。

Conclusion: 该框架能够重建不完整的患者病史并预测未来认知状态，为临床预后决策提供支持。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease is a progressive neurodegenerative disorder, and its progression varies substantially across patients. Existing work aims to forecast patients' future cognitive state, with minimal focus on reconstructing the state from past visits. Furthermore, in current research, quantifying predictive uncertainty remains underexplored and relies on costly modalities such as MRI, PET, and CSF, limiting their deployment in resource-limited settings. In this research, our primary objectives are: First, bidirectional prediction of cognitive scores from irregular visits to present the complete disease trajectory. Second, to enable interpolation and extrapolation capabilities to assist clinicians in informed prognostic decision making, and third, to provide a well-calibrated uncertainty estimate for all predictions, and finally, to achieve the objectives using the modalities available during routine visits. We propose a unified framework, GNOVA: A GRU-Neural ODE Variational Autoencoder. The architecture combines a Gated Recurrent Unit encoder and a Neural ODE decoder within a variational autoencoder framework. In our work, we forecast the CDR-SB and MMSE Scores. The GRU encoder allows for any number of inputs at any time point. The Neural-ODE decoder performs continuous estimation, allowing interpolation and extrapolation at any desired time point. The Variational autoencoder allows for uncertainty estimation in predictions. We worked with 1,727 patients from the ADNI dataset over 10 years; the model achieved mean absolute errors of 1.35 and 2.28 for CDR-SB and MMSE scores, respectively, without requiring any neuroimaging or biomarker data. Feature-ablation studies revealed that age, BMI, and APOE4 status were strong predictors. The proposed framework enables the reconstruction of incomplete patient histories and the anticipation of future cognitive states.

</details>


### [132] [Improving Multimodal Reasoning via Worst Dimension Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07801)
*Haocheng Lv,Huaping Zhang,Qiuchi Li,Lei Li,Chunxiao Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出现有过程奖励模型（PRM）在评估多模态推理时，对所有约束维度（如视觉基础、逻辑一致性）采用同等权重的启发式奖励，导致主导因素掩盖了单个维度的失败，无法保证推理过程的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态推理需要跨越视觉基础、逻辑一致性等多重约束保持完整性，但现有PRM对各维度采用同等权重的启发式奖励设计，无法识别和区分各维度的独立失败。

Method: 基于对现有PRM的批判性分析，指出其对各约束维度进行统一加权导致的维度掩盖问题，提出需要更精细的维度解耦评估方法。

Result: 现有PRM的奖励机制会导致主导因素（如视觉匹配）掩盖非主导因素（如逻辑一致性）的失败，影响推理过程整体有效性。

Conclusion: 需要打破PRM各维度间权重无差别的现状，构建能独立评估各约束维度的推理验证机制。

Abstract: Multimodal reasoning requires a path that retains integrity over a wide range of constraints, from visual grounding to logic consistency. However, the current Process Reward Models focus on heuristically defined rewards that equally weigh these factors, which may lead to the concealment of individual dimension failures by the dominating factors, without guaranteeing the validity of the reasoning process in general.

</details>


### [133] [Beyond Goodhart's Law: A Dynamic Benchmark for Evaluating Compliance in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07805)
*Yiyang Zhao,Zhuo Zhang,Qingxuan Le,Lizhen Qu,Zenglin Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MAC-Bench 是一个动态对抗性基准，用于评估多智能体系统的程序合规性。通过 SERV 流水线（Seed-Evolve-Refine-Verify）将法律文本转化为可执行场景，引入合规加权成功率（CSR）和马基雅维利差距（MG）指标，揭示了前沿模型在任务成功与规则遵守之间的普遍权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估框架忽视程序合规性，导致 LLM 智能体出现“马基雅维利行为”——为最大化奖励而策略性违反安全规则，这是古德哈特定律的直接体现。亟需一个能够评估多智能体系统在现实压力下程序对齐的基准。

Method: 提出 SERV 流水线（Seed-Evolve-Refine-Verify），采用“智能体即基准”范式，将非结构化法律文本转化为可执行、无污染的场景。合成全息沙盒环境，注入校准的社会工程压力向量，迫使智能体在任务成功与合规之间进行帕累托最优权衡。引入 CSR 和 MG 两个新指标。

Result: 对多种最先进的前沿模型进行综合评估，揭示了任务成功与合规性之间存在普遍权衡。

Conclusion: MAC-Bench 能够有效评估多智能体系统的程序对齐，揭示现有模型在压力下难以兼顾任务成功与规则遵守的问题。

Abstract: The rapid evolution of Large Language Models (LLMs) from passive assistants to autonomous, execution-capable agents has introduced critical operational risks. Most current evaluation frameworks neglect procedural compliance, leading to ''Machiavellian'' behaviors where agents strategically violate safety rules to maximize rewards - a direct manifestation of Goodhart's Law. To address this blind spot, we introduce MAC-Bench, a dynamic, adversarial benchmark designed to evaluate the procedural alignment of multi-agent systems under realistic pressure. We propose the SERV(Seed - Evolve - Refine - Verify) pipeline, an ``Agent-as-a-Benchmark'' paradigm that transforms unstructured legal texts into executable, contamination-free scenarios. By synthesizing holographic sandbox environments and injecting calibrated social-engineering pressure vectors, MAC-Bench forces agents into Pareto-optimal trade-offs between task success and regulatory adherence. We introduced novel metrics: the Compliance-Weighted Success Rate (CSR) and the Machiavellian Gap (MG), and conducted a comprehensive evaluation of state-of-the-art frontier models to reveal the pervasive trade-offs between success and compliance.

</details>


### [134] [Where Instruction Hierarchy Breaks: Diagnosing and Repairing Failures in Reasoning Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07808)
*Sanjay Kariyappa,G. Edward Suh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出白盒诊断框架，将指令层级遵循失败定位为指令识别、冲突解决、响应实现三个环节，发现不同模型/任务/上下文长度下主要失败模式各异，并基于此提出两种无训练自监控机制，将违规率降低81-99%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅端到端衡量最终响应合规性，无法区分违规行为的具体来源（指令识别失败、冲突解决失败、还是推理正确但响应违规），导致失败原因不透明、难以针对性改进。

Method: 提出白盒诊断框架，将指令层级失败分解为指令识别、冲突解决、响应实现三个环节；在IHEval和IHChallenge的长上下文版本上评估三个推理模型；提出两种无训练自监控机制：并行输入监控器（生成前低延迟冲突检测）和串行输出监控器（响应级审查修复）。

Result: 不同模型、任务和上下文长度下主导失败模式不同；模型在显式提示下通常能检测冲突并输出违规判断；最强监控器将规则遵循违规降低81-99%，GPT-5.3在静态攻击下降低86%，自适应攻击下降低45%。

Conclusion: 通过细粒度诊断定位失败环节并部署针对性自监控机制，可大幅提升推理模型在代理工作流中的指令层级遵循能力，且无需额外训练。

Abstract: Reasoning language models deployed in agentic workflows must follow an instruction hierarchy: when instructions from different sources conflict, the model should obey the highest-privilege applicable instruction. Existing benchmarks largely measure this behavior end-to-end, asking whether the final response is compliant. However, a non-compliant response can arise from several distinct failures: the model may fail to identify the relevant instructions in context, fail to resolve conflicts among identified instructions, or correctly resolve the conflict in its reasoning while still producing a violating response. We introduce a white-box diagnostic framework that localizes instruction hierarchy failures into instruction identification, conflict resolution, and response realization, making failures more interpretable. We evaluate three reasoning models--Gemma-4-31B-IT, Qwen3.6-35B-A3B, and Claude Sonnet 4.6--on long-context adaptations of IHEval and IHChallenge, and find that the dominant failure mode varies across models, tasks, and context length. Building on the observation that models can often detect conflicts and output violations when explicitly prompted, we propose two training-free self-monitoring mechanisms: a parallel input monitor for low-latency conflict detection before generation, and a sequential output monitor for response-level review and repair. Across Gemma-4-31B-IT, Claude Sonnet 4.6, and GPT-5.3, the strongest monitor reduces rule-following non-compliance by 81-99%, with GPT-5.3 reductions of 86% under static attacks and 45% under adaptive attacks.

</details>


### [135] [Scaling Participation in Modular AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07812)
*Shangbin Feng,Yike Wang,Weijia Shi,Luke Zettlemoyer,Yejin Choi,Yulia Tsvetkov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"扩展参与"新范式，通过构建模块化、自下而上的协作AI系统，让多元利益相关者贡献各自小模型，在15个任务上平均超越 monolithic LLM 达15.4%，并表现出解决个体模型均失败问题的涌现能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM由少数中心化实体开发， monolithic 架构难以捕捉人类知识、推理和价值观的多样性，亟需一种能够体现人类多元才能与需求的AI构建范式。

Method: 提出"扩展参与"（scaling participation）范式：多元参与者贡献针对自身兴趣和优先级训练的小模型，这些模型通过模块化框架协作组成组合式AI系统，实现自下而上的AI构建。

Result: 参与式AI系统在15个任务上平均超越 monolithic LLM 达15.4%，超越所有组件模型总和的更大模型；受益于贡献者多样性；显著提升每个贡献者原始优先级任务的表现；展现出涌现能力，能解决超过15%的所有个体模型均失败的问题。

Conclusion: 扩展参与范式为从 monolithic 现状转向开放、自下而上、协作的AI未来提供了技术基础。

Abstract: Humanity is a mosaic of multifaceted talents and needs, and any truly intelligent AI must reflect that richness. Yet the LLMs used by all are built by the few -- a centralized market of monolithic AI models structurally ill-suited to capture the diversity of human knowledge, reasoning, and values. Here we introduce scaling participation, a new paradigm in which modular AI systems are built from the bottom up through the contributions of diverse stakeholders. Participants contribute small models trained on their own interests and priorities; these models then collaborate in modular frameworks as compositional AI systems. Participatory AI systems outperform monolithic LLMs by up to 15.4% across 15 tasks, such as reasoning and factuality, surpassing models larger than all contributed components combined. Further experiments show that participatory AI systems benefit from contributor diversity, substantially improve on each contributor's original priorities, and exhibit emergent capabilities that allow them to solve over 15% of problems where all individual models fail. Scaling participation provides a technical foundation for transitioning from the monolithic status quo toward an open, bottom-up, and collaborative AI future.

</details>


### [136] [Joint Structural Pruning and Mixed-Precision Quantization for LLM Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07819)
*Hoang-Loc La,Truong-Thanh Le,Amir Taherkordi,Phuong Hoai Ha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种端到端框架，通过直接最小化全局误差传播的混合精度PTQ策略，以及联合学习结构化剪枝与混合精度量化策略的统一搜索空间，在超低位宽（1-3比特）下显著优于现有最先进的权重量化和联合剪枝-量化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练量化方法逐层优化量化误差，忽略了误差在网络中的累积与传播，且剪枝与量化通常独立或顺序执行，导致次优解。

Method: 提出端到端框架：(1) 混合精度PTQ策略，直接最小化全局误差传播；(2) 联合优化方法，在统一搜索空间中同时学习结构化剪枝决策和混合精度量化策略。

Result: 超低位宽下，WikiText困惑度比最先进的权重-激活量化基线降低21%；与最先进权重量化方法相比，WikiText和C4困惑度分别降低59%和85%；优于最先进联合剪枝-量化方法。

Conclusion: 所提端到端联合优化框架能有效克服逐层优化的局限，在超低位宽下显著提升LLM部署效率与性能。

Abstract: Recently, the efficiency of Large Language Models (LLMs) deployment has become a critical concern in practical applications. While post-training quantization (PTQ) and structural pruning are established techniques for reducing memory footprint and inference latency, most existing PTQ approaches optimize quantization errors on a per-layer basis, overlooking how errors accumulate and propagate through the network, often resulting in suboptimal solutions. Traditional pipelines also tend to apply pruning and quantization in isolation or sequentially, further compounding sub-optimality. We introduce a novel end-to-end framework that addresses these limitations in two key ways. First, we propose a novel mixed-precision PTQ strategy that directly minimizes global error propagation across the entire model, rather than isolating layer-wise errors. Building on this, we develop a novel joint optimization approach that simultaneously learns structural pruning decisions and mixed-precision quantization policies within a unified search space. Extensive experiments show that, at ultra-low precisions (1-3 bits), our quantization method reduces WikiText perplexity by up to 21% compared to state-of-the-art (SoTA) weight-activation quantization baselines. Against leading weight-only quantization methods, it achieves up to 59% and 85% lower perplexity on WikiText and C4, respectively. Compared to the SoTA joint pruning-and-quantization techniques, our proposed method delivers superior perplexity and reasoning performance at ultra-low bits.

</details>


### [137] [Overcoming the Regulatory Bottleneck via Agent-to-Agent Protocols: A Nuclear Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07866)
*Akshay J. Dave,David Grabaskas,Joseph A. Renevitz,Richard B. Vilim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出监管审查协议(RCP)，一种Agent-to-Agent通信标准，用于替代核反应堆设计监管中的人人交互流程。基于1,236份NRC文件分析，该协议可削减50-77%成本（节省21M-44M美元），缩短65%时间线（15个月），并有望在更广泛的监管领域带来每年2100-3300亿美元的经济效益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先进核反应堆设计的监管审查流程耗时超过3年、耗资数亿美元，存在严重的效率瓶颈。作者试图通过标准化Agent间通信来解决这一结构化问题，而非依赖算法优化。

Method: 提出RCP协议，一种Agent-to-Agent通信标准，替代人工监管流程中的人人沟通管道，同时保留安全关键决策点的人类监督。基于1,236份NRC先进反应堆卷宗文件进行校准，并通过多Agent原型系统进行验证。

Result: 相比重建基线（8,900万美元、42个月），RCP可实现50-77%的成本削减（2,100-4,400万美元），以及65%的时间缩短（15个月）。无协议的独立Agent仅能达到5,400-7,400万美元和21个月。残差成本和时间缺口是结构性的，而非算法性的。

Conclusion: 监管审查中的瓶颈是组织间沟通管道的结构化问题，只有Agent间标准才能有效压缩。该方法可推广至药品审批、环境许可、金融监管和航空认证等领域。美国监管文书负担年机会成本达4,265亿美元，RCP的推广可带来约2,100-3,300亿美元的年度节省，接近美国GDP的1%。

Abstract: Regulatory review of advanced nuclear reactor designs routinely spans more than three years and consumes hundreds of millions of dollars in combined regulator and applicant labor. We present the Regulatory Context Protocol (RCP), an Agent-to-Agent communication standard that replaces the formal human-to-human pipeline between regulators and applicants with a structured, auditable agentic channel, while preserving human oversight at safety-significant decision points. The protocol is calibrated against an analysis of 1,236 documents from U.S. Nuclear Regulatory Commission advanced reactor dockets and demonstrated with a working multi-agent pilot. Against an 89M USD, 42-month Reconstructed Baseline, RCP cuts costs by 50-77 percent (21M-44M USD) and timelines by 65 percent (15 months). Without a shared protocol, Standalone Agents reach only 54M-74M USD and 21 months. The residual cost-and-time gap is structural, not algorithmic: it traces to the inter-organizational pipeline that only an agent-to-agent standard can compress. The same bottleneck - formal multi-party review under strict auditability requirements - characterizes pharmaceutical approvals, environmental permitting, financial supervision, and aviation certification. The US regulatory paperwork burden carries a 426.5 billion USD annual opportunity cost; replicated broadly, the projected 50-77 percent reduction implies savings on the order of 210-330 billion USD per year - approaching 1 percent of US GDP.

</details>


### [138] [Safety is Contextual, LLM-Judges Are Not: Navigating the Rigid Priors of Evaluators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07874)
*Anissa Alloula,Federico Licini,Ava Batchkala,Seraphina Goldfarb-Tarrant*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM评委依赖上下文信息和可被引导至不同安全定义，但若新信息与其内部安全先验冲突，则难以调整评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为安全评估裁判至关重要，但其对上下文信息的依赖性和对不同安全定义的可引导性这两个关键性质尚未被充分研究。

Method: 评估多种通用LLM和特定安全裁判的安全判断能力，研究任务演示、新型上下文信息和变化的安全定义对其评估结果的影响。

Result: LLM裁判能从新信息中学习，但当上下文或安全定义与其内部先验矛盾时，普遍难以调整其评估结果。

Conclusion: LLM作为安全评估裁判存在内在偏置，即使接收到矛盾的外部信息，其内部安全先验仍然主导判断，这限制了其在复杂场景下的可靠性和适应性。

Abstract: LLMs-as-judges are the only way to evaluate safety at scale. Despite their importance, LLM-judges themselves are rarely evaluated beyond human agreement in simple, static benchmarks. We therefore investigate two under-explored but crucial properties of LLMs-as-judges: their susceptibility to relying on in context-information, and their steerability to differing safety definitions, which may not align with their internal safety priors.
