Abstract: When a language model trains on its own verified outputs, does it acquire capability beyond its base, or merely get better at expressing capability the base already had? We make the question decidable with a teacher-free "constellation" -- a generator, a learned critic, and a free exact verifier -- on a FlashFill-style "trapdoor" DSL, where verified (problem, solution) pairs are cheap to synthesize, hard to invert, and free to check exactly. Everything runs on one 4-bit Qwen3-4B on a single 24 GB GPU, with no model in the loop larger than the base. We report three findings. (i) Critic-guided selection beats verifier-filtered best-of-$k$ by $+9.1$ pp ($6/6$ seeds), with the entire gain localized to tasks where candidates disagree on held-out inputs. (ii) Per-round STaR self-training raises the ceiling but never accelerates -- the gain tracks remaining headroom and decelerates across $K=4$ independent training trajectories. (iii) The domain has no clean zero-capability frontier, so the usual "$0\% \to$ climb $=$ emergence" test is invalid here. A measured pass@$K$ crossover settles the diagnosis: the trained model wins at the operating budget (pass@$8$) but the base overtakes it at a large budget (pass@$64$) on every trajectory, so self-training concentrates probability mass rather than expanding reach. This is amplification, not compounding. ($K=4$ is indicative, not yet a robust across-trajectory CI.)

</details>


### [318] [Instrumented data for causal scientific machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07865)
*Daniel N. Wilke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“仪器化数据”作为科学机器学习的新数据范式，即每个数据点附带产生它的机制模型、显式不确定性及可执行的反事实族，通过V&V仪器化图像到模拟管线实现，支持因果干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学机器学习受限于数据质量：观测数据只记录结果不记录原因，模板合成数据仅适用于特定模板而非用户面临的实际案例。需要一种新的数据范式来弥补这一鸿沟。

Method: 提出“仪器化数据”概念，通过验证与确认（V&V）仪器化图像到模拟管线实现：传感器观测转化为完全指定的、有求解器支持的模拟，带有显式可编辑参数及传播的偶然性/认知性不确定性，支持通过Pearl的do算子进行因果干预。

Result: 该数据载体是案例特定的、机制监督的，支持因果干预；在计算生物学、气候、材料、流体力学和医学成像等领域具有验证、审计和替代模型训练的近期意义；长期可证伪的含义涉及科学推理的基础模型。

Conclusion: 仪器化数据在操作层面已可行，有望解决科学ML的数据瓶颈，为从观测到因果推理的桥梁提供新路径。

Abstract: Scientific machine learning is limited less by model size than by the data it is trained on. Observational data records what happened but not why; template synthetic data has a known generating process but only for the simulator's template, not the case a user faces. We argue a third option is now operationally feasible: instrumented data, in which every datum carries the mechanistic model that produced it, an explicit uncertainty over that model, and an executable family of counterfactuals. Verification-and-validation (V&V) instrumented image-to-simulation pipelines are one realisation: a sensor observation becomes a fully specified, solver-backed simulation with explicit, editable parameters and a propagated aleatoric/epistemic uncertainty. The substrate is case-specific, mechanistically supervised, and supports causal interventions through Pearl's do-operator. Near-term consequences for validation, auditing, and surrogate training span computational biology, climate, materials, fluid mechanics, and medical imaging; a longer-term, falsifiable implication concerns foundation models for scientific reasoning.

</details>


### [319] [Still: Amortized KV Cache Compaction in a Single Forward Pass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07878)
*Charles O'Neill,Alex Sandomirsky,Harry Partridge,Mudith Jayasekara,Max Kirkby*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Still 是一种轻量级 Perceiver 模型，通过单次前向传播为长上下文 LLM 生成紧凑的 KV 缓存，在 8× 到 200× 压缩率和 8k 到 128k 上下文长度下均优于现有方法，且支持迭代压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KV 缓存是长上下文语言模型部署的内存瓶颈。现有压缩方法（选择法轻量但受限于子集，合成法表达力强但依赖每上下文优化）无法同时满足轻量、表达力强和跨轨迹可重用三个要求。

Method: 提出 Still，一种在每个 Transformer 层插入的小型 Perceiver 模型，针对冻结的基座模型一次性训练，通过单次前向传播生成紧凑的键和值，无需每上下文优化。

Result: 在 Qwen 和 Gemma 模型上，Still 在 8× 到 200× 压缩率和 8k 到 128k 上下文长度下占据速度-质量前沿；在 RULER 基准上超过最强基线 8-22 分；在 HELMET 上保持全上下文的优势，在 LongBench 摘要任务中优于 KV-Distill。

Conclusion: 摊销（amortization）使长上下文缓存压缩变得可行，合成方法使紧凑状态在极端压缩下保持有用。Still 可迭代应用，支持长时域场景。

Abstract: The KV cache is the memory bottleneck of long-horizon language model deployment. Practically, a deployable compactor must be lightweight enough to call during inference, expressive enough to preserve context under constraint, and reusable across a trajectory. Existing compaction methods satisfy only part of this requirement: selection methods are lightweight but subset-bound, while synthesis methods are expressive but rely on per-context optimization. Here we introduce Still, a small per-layer Perceiver trained once against a frozen base model that produces compact keys and values in a single forward pass. On Qwen and Gemma models, Still occupies the favorable side of the speed--quality frontier across compression ratios from $8\times$ to $200\times$ and context lengths from $8$k to $128$k. On the long-context RULER grid, Still exceeds the strongest baseline by 8--22 points. The same compact cache also supports free-form summarization, preserving most of the full-context gain on HELMET and winning a pairwise LongBench summarization comparison against KV-Distill. Because compaction is a forward pass, Still can be applied iteratively, entering a long-horizon regime unavailable to per-context methods. We show that amortization makes long-context cache compaction tractable, and synthesis makes its compact state useful at extreme compression.

</details>


### [320] [Breaking the Bubble: Asynchronous Pipeline Parallel Training with Bounded Weight Inconsistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07881)
*Itay Elam,Eliron Rahimi,Avi Mendelson,Chaim Baskin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PACI是一种无气泡的异步流水线并行方法，通过局部梯度累积控制版本漂移，在不使用权重暂存、预测或全局同步的情况下，实现了同步流水线的稳定性和异步流水线的吞吐量，训练速度提升最高达1.69倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流水线并行调度在吞吐量、内存和优化一致性之间存在权衡：同步流水线保持权重一致性但存在气泡，异步流水线消除气泡但引入权重版本不匹配问题，通常需要额外的权重暂存、预测或修正机制。

Method: 提出局部梯度累积作为版本控制机制，通过减缓参数版本演化速度使其相对于流水线延迟更慢，从而限制任何微批次跨越的优化器更新次数，同时保持稳态利用率。

Result: 在GPT风格语言模型预训练中，PACI匹配了同步1F1B-flush的稳定性和最终困惑度，保持相同峰值内存，实现完全利用的流水线吞吐量，训练时间最高提升1.69倍。

Conclusion: 前向/后向不一致性无需完全消除：当被显式约束时，可以安全地将其转化为显著的效率提升。

Abstract: Pipeline parallelism is essential for training large neural networks, but existing schedules trade off throughput, memory, and optimization consistency. Synchronous pipelines preserve forward/backward weight consistency but suffer from bubbles; asynchronous pipelines remove bubbles but introduce weight-version mismatch, typically requiring weight stashing, prediction, or correction mechanisms. We introduce PACI (Pipeline Asynchronous training with Controlled Inconsistency), a bubble-free asynchronous pipeline method that bounds forward/backward version drift without weight stashing, prediction, additional parameter copies, or global synchronization. The key idea is to use local gradient accumulation as a version-control mechanism: by slowing parameter-version evolution relative to pipeline delay, PACI limits the number of optimizer updates crossed by any micro-batch while preserving steady-state utilization. In GPT-style language-model pretraining, PACI matches the stability and final perplexity of synchronous 1F1B-flush, retains the same peak memory footprint, achieves fully utilized pipeline throughput, and improves training time-to-accuracy by up to $1.69\times$ over the fastest flush baseline. These results show that forward/backward inconsistency need not be eliminated: when explicitly bounded, it can be safely traded for substantial efficiency gains.

</details>


### [321] [Strained Coherence: A Pre-Failure Signal in Coding Agent Execution Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07889)
*Marut Pandya,Kasey Zhang,Baiqing Lyu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“紧张连贯”（strained coherence）这一安全相关失败模式：LLM编码体在推理中识别到问题后仍继续行动。作者构建了Claude Sonnet 4.6检测器，在Terminal-bench-2轨迹上评估，被标记轨迹失败率94% vs 未标记的46%（47点差距）。检测器在匹配选择性下精度达94%，优于词法基线的88%。跨模型复制结果方向一致但显著性受思维内容稀疏性影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全研究中，模型在推理中明确说出问题却仍执行错误行为这一模式缺乏系统定义和检测方法。作者希望形式化定义“紧张连贯”这一失败模式，并构建可扩展的自动检测工具。

Method: 1) 给出“紧张连贯”的操作性定义；2) 构建Claude Sonnet 4.6评判器，读取完整轨迹并标记模式发生片段；3) 在Qwen3.5-35B-A3B的44条Terminal-bench-2轨迹上评估；4) 在Gemma4-31B的43条轨迹上复制验证；5) 进行消融分析（思维内容稀疏性、困惑程度分层）；6) 进行释义鲁棒性测试（8/8轨迹）；7) 与词法话语标记基线对比。

Result: 被标记轨迹失败率94% vs 未标记46%（47点差距，p=0.003）；排除3条提示嵌入样本后46点差距（p=0.006）。匹配选择性下检测器精度94% vs 词法基线88%。两种方法交叉的10条轨迹失败率100%（95% CI [69%,100%]）。Gemma复制：方向一致但整体不显著（20点差距，p=0.31），受13条零思维内容轨迹拖累。高困惑度Gemma三分位差距+30点；Qwen中、高困惑度三分位各+40点。首次标记出现在轨迹83-84%时间点。释义测试8/8通过。

Conclusion: “紧张连贯”是可检测的安全失败模式，检测器优于简单词法基线，输出可解释的片段级信息（引用承认、引用行动、类型化冲突）。但检测效果受模型思维内容稀疏性制约，在低困惑度场景下信号衰减。该方法为LLM安全监控提供了超越单一预测变量的细粒度分析工具。

Abstract: LLM-based coding agents sometimes acknowledge a problem in their own reasoning and then proceed anyway. We call this pattern strained coherence: a safety-relevant failure mode in which an agent has information that should change its behavior, states that information, and still acts against it. The pattern overlaps with verbalized reward hacking, where an agent names a tension between a task proxy and the underlying goal yet optimizes the proxy anyway. We give an operational definition, build a Claude Sonnet 4.6 judge that reads full trajectories and flags spans where the pattern occurs, and evaluate it on 44 Terminal-bench-2 trajectories using a Qwen3.5-35B-A3B backbone. Flagged trajectories fail 94% of the time versus 46% for unflagged trajectories (47-point gap, Fisher's exact p = 0.003; 46 points after excluding three prompt-embedded examples, p = 0.006). At matched selectivity, the detector reaches 94% precision versus 88% for a lexical discourse-marker baseline; the 10-trajectory intersection of the two methods has a 100% failure rate (Clopper-Pearson 95% CI [69%, 100%]). We replicate on Gemma4-31B with 43 trajectories: the overall signal is directionally consistent but not significant (20-point gap, p = 0.31), with attenuation driven largely by 13 trajectories with zero think content, where the detector has no substrate to analyze. In the high-verbosity Gemma tertile, the gap is +30 points; in the mid- and high-verbosity Qwen tertiles, it is +40 points each. The first flag appears at a median of 83-84% of elapsed trajectory time across both models, and the binary flag survives paraphrases that soften explicit conflict markers (8/8 trajectories). Unlike univariate predictors, the detector emits interpretable span-level output -- quoted acknowledgment, quoted action, and typed conflict -- showing what the agent saw and ignored.

</details>


### [322] [Partially Performative Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07890)
*Jaewook Lee,Tijana Zrnic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"部分执行性预测"框架，统一考虑预测模型部署引起的内生性分布偏移（模型影响数据分布）和外生性分布偏移（外部环境随时间变化），并分析在线学习算法在此环境下的收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统执行性预测仅考虑模型对数据分布的内生影响，但现实场景中分布偏移往往是模型影响与外部环境变化共同作用的结果。现有研究缺乏一个同时建模这两种偏移的统一框架。

Method: 将执行性预测推广为部分执行性预测，允许数据分布同时随部署模型和外部时变过程演化；定义在线执行性稳定性和最优性的概念；分析重复再训练等启发式学习算法的收敛行为。

Result: 建立了部分执行性环境下在线稳定性和最优性的形式化定义，刻画了重复再训练等启发式方法在此类混合偏移环境中成功适应的条件。

Conclusion: 部分执行性预测为理解现实世界中模型与环境的动态交互提供了更全面的理论框架，弥合了经典分布偏移理论与执行性预测之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Performative prediction studies feedback loops that arise when predictive models are deployed in consequential domains. In these settings, deploying a model can change the population whose patterns the model aims to predict, inducing a distribution shift that is endogenous to the learning system. This perspective departs from classical treatments of distribution shift, where shifts are typically modeled as exogenous changes in the data-generating process. Yet, in practice, distribution shift is rarely one or the other. Predictive models may influence future data through the decisions they support, while the world itself continues to drift for reasons beyond the learner's control. We study partially performative prediction, a framework that captures both endogenous and exogenous sources of distribution shift. The framework generalizes performative prediction by allowing the data distribution to evolve both in response to the deployed model and according to an external, time-varying process. We extend the central notions of performative stability and performative optimality to this setting by defining their online analogues that track the evolving partially performative environment. We analyze practical learning heuristics, including repeated retraining, and characterize when they successfully adapt to partially performative environments.

</details>


### [323] [Temporal Coverage over Density: Parsimonious Training-Set Design for ML Climate Downscaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07898)
*Karandeep Singh,Stefan Rahimi,Chad W. Thackeray,Stephen Cropper,Alex Hall*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在固定计算预算下，用于训练机器学习降尺度模型的高分辨率气候模拟年份，应分布在整个气候轨迹中（同时包含历史和未来年份），而非集中在连续历史时期，以更好地捕捉强迫气候响应和内部变率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率区域气候模拟计算成本高昂，需要开发机器学习降尺度器和模拟器，但如何最优分配有限的高分辨率模拟数据以同时捕获强迫气候响应和内部变率是一个关键挑战。

Method: 使用CESM2大型集合模拟美国西部地区，在固定数据预算下比较三种训练年份选择策略：连续历史年份块、仅从模拟期始末抽取年份、以及在整个气候轨迹中均匀分布的年份。

Result: 包含历史和未来年份的策略优于仅用历史年份；全轨迹分布策略表现最佳；仅用十分之一的高分辨率数据仍能接近全数据训练的性能。

Conclusion: 在固定计算预算下，对气候状态的广泛采样比时间连续性更有价值，为区域气候降尺度和大集合预测工作流提供了实用指导。

Abstract: High-resolution regional climate simulations provide critical information for climate impacts assessments but remain computationally expensive, motivating the development of machine-learning downscalers and emulators. A key challenge is determining how limited high-resolution simulations should be distributed across a changing climate trajectory to capture both forced climate response and internal variability. Using the CESM2 Large Ensemble over the western United States, we compare three training-year selection strategies under fixed data budgets: a contiguous block of historical years, years drawn from both the beginning and end of the simulation period, and years distributed throughout the full climate trajectory. Including both historical and future years consistently outperforms training on historical years alone, demonstrating the importance of exposing downscaling models to climate states outside the historical record and highlighting limitations of stationarity assumptions common in statistical downscaling. Training on years distributed throughout the full climate trajectory performs best overall, indicating that broad sampling of internal variability provides additional information beyond exposure to the forced climate response alone. Models trained on temporally distributed subsets more successfully reproduce variability in unseen ensemble members while retaining strong performance across a wide range of climate diagnostics. Even when trained on only one-tenth of the available high-resolution years, temporally distributed models remain highly competitive with full-data training. These results suggest that, under fixed computational budgets, broad sampling of climate states is more valuable than temporal continuity when allocating scarce high-resolution simulations. The findings provide practical guidance for regional climate downscaling and large-ensemble projection workflows.

</details>


### [324] [Layer-wise Derivative Controlled Networks Achieve Competitive Accuracy and Gradient Stability Across Data Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07908)
*Rowan Martnishn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于ChainzRule的导数控制网络，通过三次多项式层与前向模式逐层雅可比惩罚(DREG)实现梯度调控。实验表明，该方法在Pima糖尿病数据集上从5%到100%训练数据中均保持稳定的准确率优势，梯度尾部比率(1.01-1.02 vs ReLU网络的1.07-1.09)极为稳定；在SST-5数据集上，无论冻结嵌入还是BERT微调场景均达到或超越先前基线。统计显著(p<0.05)说明层式导数控制诱导了面向低频稳定表示的归纳偏置，梯度尾部比率可作为可靠的、免标签的泛化诊断指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索基于链式法则的导数控制网络在不同数据规模、不同领域(表格数据与NLP)下的泛化特性，验证层式导数惩罚是否成为一种普适的诱导偏置机制。

Method: 采用三次多项式层(CR)结合轻量级前向模式逐层雅可比惩罚(DREG)，通过消融实验研究DREG系数调度形状对性能的影响，并在表格数据(Pima Diabetes)和NLP数据(SST-5)上对比基线方法。

Result: CR在Pima Diabetes数据集上从低数据到全数据均优于基线，梯度尾部比率极稳定(1.01-1.02)；在SST-5上，无论冻结嵌入还是微调BERT，均取得竞争性或更优结果，且统计显著(p<0.05)。

Conclusion: 层式导数控制引入了一种面向低频稳定表示的归纳偏置，能够在表格和NLP领域、不同数据量和表示质量下鲁棒泛化；梯度尾部比率可作为免标签的泛化能力诊断指标。

Abstract: Derivative-controlled networks based on ChainzRule (CR) combine cubic polynomial layers with a lightweight forward-mode per-layer Jacobian penalty (DREG). In this second paper of a multi-part series, we evaluate the generalization properties of CR across data regimes.
  We ablate the shape of the DREG coefficient schedule, demonstrating that the optimal annealing range depends on representation noise. On the Pima Diabetes dataset, CR achieves strong low-data performance and maintains a consistent accuracy advantage over baselines from 5\% to 100\% training data, supported by exceptionally stable gradient tail ratios ($\sim$1.01--1.02 vs. 1.07--1.09 for ReLU networks). Extensions to SST-5 show competitive or superior results in both frozen-embedding and BERT fine-tuned regimes, including outperforming prior BERT baselines despite substantially less training data. These results are statistically significant: CR achieves superior accuracy over the strongest published baselines we could identify on both datasets ($p < 0.05$).
  These results establish that layer-wise derivative control induces a structural inductive bias toward low-frequency, stable representations that generalizes robustly across tabular and NLP domains, data volumes, and representation qualities. The gradient tail ratio serves as a reliable, label-free diagnostic of generalization capability.

</details>


### [325] [CAAL: Contextual Bandits based Online Hand-Craft Active Learning Strategy Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07910)
*Shao-An Yin,Jiacong Li,Tianpei Xie,Cecile Levasseur,Wojciech Kowalinski,Nicola Elia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出上下文自适应主动学习（CAAL），通过多臂赌博机框架动态选择标注策略，利用外部上下文信息预测奖励，在公共数据集上优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动学习算法中未标注数据的统计分布不确定，难以选择最佳的手工策略。

Method: 将每个手工策略视为一个"臂"，利用外部上下文信息预测奖励，动态选择批次标注策略，支持领域知识定制奖励与上下文候选。

Result: 在公共数据集上，CAAL优于现有基线自适应策略，且结果在不同批次大小下保持一致。

Conclusion: CAAL框架通过上下文感知的奖励预测实现了比仅依赖已标注数据反馈的策略更优的主动学习效果。

Abstract: The challenge with active learning algorithms is the uncertainty of the statistical distribution of unlabeled data, making it difficult to choose the best hand-crafted strategy. To address this, we introduced Contextual Adaptive Active Learning (CAAL). In CAAL, each "arm" represents a hand-crafted strategy. Unlike existing frameworks that select strategies based only on feedback from labeled data, we dynamically choose strategies for labeling batches of data using reward prediction with external context information. This general framework allows for customization with domain knowledge to design more effective rewards and context candidates. In addition, we experimentally show that CAAL outperforms the existing baseline adaptive strategy on public datasets using our reward and context design. Our results are consistent regardless of batch size in each iteration.

</details>


### [326] [The Easy, the Hard, and the Learnable: Confidence and Difficulty-Adaptive Policy Optimization for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07950)
*Zhanke Zhou,Xiangyu Lu,Chentao Cao,Brando Miranda,Tongliang Liu,Bo Han,Sanmi Koyejo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GRPO训练中简单、困难与可学习问题被统一处理导致计算效率低下。通过分析token对数概率、组归一化优势和更新权重，发现置信度膨胀、优势收缩和层次收敛三种动态。据此提出CoDaPO方法，利用滚动置信度和经验难度对问题赋权，重加权策略更新并重采样高价值可学习问题，在12个基准上持续提升精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准GRPO风格训练对简单、困难和可学习问题采用统一采样和加权，导致计算分配效率低下，缺乏对问题难度与模型当前能力的自适应机制。

Method: 首先通过追踪token对数概率、组归一化优势和token级更新权重，揭示训练中的三种动态（置信度膨胀、优势收缩、层次收敛）。然后提出CoDaPO，基于滚动置信度和经验难度为每个问题计算有界价值，据此重加权策略更新并在小批量内重采样高价值可学习问题。

Result: 在12个基准上，CoDaPO相比现有RL方法持续提升准确率。

Conclusion: 基于置信度和难度自适应的问题加权与重采样策略，能够在固定计算预算下更有效地发现可学习问题，显著提升LLM推理性能。

Abstract: RL with verifiable rewards can substantially improve LLM reasoning, yet standard GRPO-style training often treats easy, hard, and learnable questions alike through uniform sampling and weighting, leading to inefficient compute allocation. We study GRPO by tracking token log-probabilities, group-normalized advantages, and the induced token-level update weights. This reveals three recurring dynamics as training proceeds: (1) confidence inflation, (2) advantage contraction, and (3) hierarchical convergence. These findings suggest that the utility of each update depends strongly on both question difficulty and the model's current competence. Motivated by this, we propose Confidence and Difficulty-adaptive Policy Optimization (CoDaPO), which assigns each question a bounded value from rollout confidence and empirical difficulty. CoDaPO then uses this value to reweight policy updates and resample high-value learnable questions within mini-batches, thereby increasing discovery within the learnable band under a fixed compute budget. Across twelve benchmarks, CoDaPO consistently improves accuracy over existing RL methods. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/tmlr-group/CoDaPO.

</details>


### [327] [Minibatch Selection via Partition Matroid Constrained Gradient Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07954)
*Prayas Agrawal,Prateek Chanda,Ishita Khatri,Ganesh Ramakrishnan,Bamdev Mishra,Pratik Jawanpuria*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PartitionSel方法，通过分区拟阵约束下的验证引导梯度匹配效用实现跨领域小批量选择，在Qwen2.5和Llama-3微调中优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构数据上训练LLM时，现有小批量选择方法要么在各领域内独立采样，要么依赖计算昂贵的代理模型学习连续领域权重，缺乏高效的跨领域耦合选择机制。

Method: 提出PartitionSel，在分区拟阵约束的每领域预算下，最大化验证引导的梯度匹配效用函数，实现跨领域小批量选择。该目标是弱子模的，可采用正交匹配追踪算法求解并具有可证明的近似保证。

Result: 在MetaMathQA和Mol-Instructions基准上微调Qwen2.5和Llama-3，PartitionSel在收敛速度和覆盖度上均优于单领域和领域无关的基线方法，并减少了批次内的梯度冲突对数量。

Conclusion: 跨领域耦合机制能有效减少冗余选择并生成更兼容的训练更新，为异构数据训练LLM提供了高效的小批量选择方案。

Abstract: Training large language models (LLMs) on heterogeneous data requires selecting minibatches that balance convergence speed with coverage across domains. Existing methods either select samples independently within each domain or rely on computationally expensive proxy models to learn continuous domain weights. We propose PartitionSel, a cross-domain minibatch selection approach that maximizes a validation-guided gradient-matching utility under per-domain budgets encoded as a partition-matroid constraint. By coupling the per-domain budgets through a single utility, PartitionSel is designed to reduce redundancy in selections across domains. The proposed objective is weakly submodular and admits an orthogonal matching pursuit algorithm with provable approximation guarantees. Empirically, we evaluate PartitionSel for minibatch selection during the fine-tuning of Qwen2.5 and Llama-3 on MetaMathQA and Mol-Instructions. PartitionSel achieves robust gains over per-domain and domain-agnostic baselines on both benchmarks. It also reduces the number of conflicting gradient pairs within each batch, indicating that the cross-domain coupling translates into more compatible training updates.

</details>


### [328] [Overcoming the Limits of Finite Difference Method; Physics-Informed Neural Network for Noisy High-Dimensional Heat Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07982)
*Shreesh Bhattarai,Harish Chandra Bhandari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出物理信息神经网络(PINN)框架，用于解决高维瞬态热扩散在有噪边界条件下的精度崩溃问题。在20%边界噪声、3D场景下，PINN保持约91%准确率，而有限差分法(FDM)降至36%；在铜热系统中，PINN将边界重构误差降低3.3倍。同时存在维度驱动的效率交叉：3D中PINN所需时空节点少于FDM。研究重新定义了求解器选择标准：噪声暴露与维度共同决定，在高噪声高维度下PINN应成为操作标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维瞬态热扩散在不可避免的噪声边界条件下，传统数值方法（如FDM）的精度会急剧下降，暴露了经典方法的根本局限性，亟需一种系统的解决方案。

Method: 采用物理信息神经网络(PINN)框架，在一维、二维、三维空间维度下系统性地处理含噪边界条件的热扩散问题，并与有限差分法(FDM)进行对比。

Result: 在3D场景20%边界噪声下，PINN保持约91%准确率，FDM仅36%；铜热系统中PINN边界重构误差降低3.3倍；3D中PINN所需时空节点少于FDM且精度更优。

Conclusion: 求解器选择的关键轴不仅是精度，而是噪声暴露与维度的联合考量。在高噪声高维度场景下，经典求解器范式不足，PINN应成为该场景下的操作标准。

Abstract: High-dimensional transient heat diffusion under noisy boundary conditions exposes a fundamental limitation of classical numerical methods: accuracy degrades catastrophically where physical noise is unavoidable. This paper presents a Physics-Informed Neural Network (PINN) framework as a systematic solution to this problem across one, two, and three spatial dimensions, establishing clear operational regimes that redefine solver selection in noisy thermal systems. Under 20% boundary noise in 3D, PINN sustains approximately 91% accuracy while Finite Difference Method (FDM) collapses to 36%, a clear decisive advantage. This is further confirmed in a physical copper thermal system, where PINN reduces boundary reconstruction error by 3.3 times under realistic noise conditions. This noise resilience is accompanied by a dimensionality-driven efficiency crossover: PINN requires fewer spacetime nodes than FDM in 3D while achieving superior accuracy, exposing the true cost of classical discretization at scale. These findings reframe solver selection: the decisive axis is not accuracy alone, but noise exposure and dimensionality jointly. When noise and dimensionality are both high, the classical solver paradigm is insufficient; this work provides the foundation to justify PINN as the operational standard in such regimes.

</details>


### [329] [Enhancing AI Interpretability and Safety through Localised Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07998)
*Ian Seet,Jonas Bozenhard,Simon Osterman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出局部化硬件ML架构（低带宽、高每节点表达力）相比在GPU集群上运行的深度神经网络，在小数据集场景下更具可解释性和计算效率，并评估了多种硬件ML范式在节点表达力、能效和技术成熟度方面的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI（LLMs和LRMs）在可解释性、安全性和可持续性方面存在担忧，深度神经网络虽具强大函数逼近能力，但牺牲了可解释性和计算效率。本文试图通过类比局部化ML模型在小数据集上的优势，探索局部化硬件架构能否带来类似的可解释性和效率提升。

Method: 采用类比推理方法：从局部化ML模型（小数据集上比深度神经网络更可解释和高效）出发，类比到局部化硬件ML架构。随后对多种硬件ML范式进行评估，分析其每节点表达力、能效和技术成熟度。

Result: 局部化硬件架构（低带宽、高每节点表达力）有潜力在保持小数据集竞争力的同时，从根本上比运行在GPU集群上的深度神经网络更可解释。

Conclusion: 局部化硬件ML架构在小数据集场景下是可解释性和效率的有前途方向，但需进一步评估具体技术实现和成熟度。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative AI, especially powerful Large Language Models (LLMs) and Large Reasoning Models (LRMs), raise concerns over the interpretability, safety and sustainability of these large and opaque AI models. The power of such architectures is derived not only from the scalability of deep neural networks, but also massively parallel hardware such as GPU clusters. The diffuse nature of deep neural networks gives them great function-approximation capability when provided with sufficient training data but imposes a cost in interpretability and computational efficiency. Observing that localised machine learning (ML) models tend to be more interpretable and computationally efficient than deep neural networks on small datasets, we reason by analogy that similar advantages may apply to specific localised hardware ML architectures. We argue that localised architectures with lower bandwidth but higher expressivity per node have the potential to be fundamentally more interpretable than deep neural networks running on GPU clusters while remaining competitive for smaller datasets. We then evaluate the suitability of various hardware ML paradigms for implementing such localised architectures and evaluate their per-node expressivity, energy efficiency and practical maturity of the technology required.

</details>


### [330] [Evaluating the Impact of Task Granularity on Catastrophic Forgetting in Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08013)
*Emre Alyamac,Himanshu Janmeda,Shashwat Krishna,Yash Vijay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究持续学习中知识顺序对灾难性遗忘的影响，比较了"从粗到细"、"从细到粗"和"平坦"三种学习顺序，使用弹性权重巩固（EWC）方法在CIFAR-100数据集上实验，发现先学习通用类别再学习具体类别可能减少遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习中灾难性遗忘是核心挑战，本文探索学习顺序（即模型接收信息的先后次序）如何影响知识保留能力，试图回答先学习通用类别再学习具体类别是否能减少遗忘的问题。

Method: 在CIFAR-100上设计三种学习顺序：（1）粗到细：先训练2个超类，再扩展到10个子类；（2）细到粗：先训练10个子类，再合并为2个超类；（3）平坦：从开始就训练所有10个类。所有方案使用弹性权重巩固（EWC）防止过渡时遗忘。

Result: 假设先学习通用模式能建立稳定基础，帮助模型在学习更细粒度区分时保留知识。评估使用准确率、精确率、召回率、F1以及后向迁移和遗忘率等持续学习指标。

Conclusion: 该工作可为需要增量学习的实际系统设计学习序列提供指导，说明合理的学习顺序有助于缓解灾难性遗忘问题。

Abstract: Catastrophic forgetting, the abrupt loss of previously acquired knowledge upon learning new information, remains the central challenge in Continual Learning. This project investigates whether the order in which a model learns information affects how well it retains knowledge. Specifically, we ask: does learning general categories first (like "animals" vs "vehicles") before learning specific classes (like "dog" vs "cat") reduce forgetting compared to learning all classes at once?
  We test three approaches on CIFAR-100: (1) Coarse-to-Fine: train on 2 super-classes, then expand to 10 specific sub-classes, (2) Fine-to-Coarse: train on 10 sub-classes, then group into 2 super-classes, and (3) Flat: train on all 10 classes from the start. We use Elastic Weight Consolidation (EWC) to prevent forgetting during transitions. Our hypothesis is that learning general patterns first creates a stable foundation that helps the model retain knowledge when learning more detailed distinctions. We evaluate using standard metrics (accuracy, precision, recall, F1) plus continual learning metrics like backward transfer and forgetting rates. This work could inform how we design learning sequences for real-world systems that need to learn incrementally.

</details>


### [331] [Semantic Quorum Assurance: Collective Certification for Non-Deterministic AI Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08021)
*Jun He,Deying Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出语义法定人数保证（SQA），一种用于治理非确定性LLM代理在云基础设施中产生突变的控制平面原语。SQA通过多样化的只读验证器面板、风险自适应法定人数谓词和执行门控，将不安全批准率从单代理的18.5%降低到0.3%，验证延迟中位数为1.45-4.12秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理集成到自动化云运维中时，分布式系统面临语义可靠性问题：代理可以生成语法有效、静态授权但操作上不安全的变更（如修改IAM策略、开放防火墙安全组等）。经典分布式共识协议仅复制确定性状态转换，不评估提议意图的安全性。

Method: 将提议表示为绑定到密码学证据链的声明式执行合约，路由到由多样化的只读、沙箱化验证器代理组成的面板进行判断；通过风险自适应法定人数谓词聚合判断，该谓词强制模型和原型多样性、根据校准保证分数调整权重、尊重原型特定否决权；通过的提议仅通过主权执行门执行。

Result: 在500个基础设施突变场景中（排除模糊场景的不安全/安全试验），SQA将不安全批准率从单代理验证的18.5%降低到0.3%，不同风险桶的验证延迟中位数为1.45-4.12秒。

Conclusion: SQA有效解决了LLM代理在云基础设施中产生不安全突变的语义可靠性问题，通过多样化的验证器面板和风险自适应聚合机制大幅降低了误审批率，同时保持了可接受的验证延迟。

Abstract: As large language model (LLM) agents are integrated into autonomous cloud operations, distributed systems face a semantic reliability problem: proposer agents can generate production mutations, such as modifying IAM policies, opening firewall security groups, or executing data exports, that are syntactically valid and statically authorized but operationally unsafe. Classical distributed consensus protocols replicate deterministic state transitions but do not evaluate the safety of the proposed intent. To address this gap, we introduce Semantic Quorum Assurance (SQA), a control-plane primitive for governing non-deterministic agentic infrastructure. SQA represents proposals as declarative execution contracts bound to cryptographic evidence chains and routes them to a diverse panel of read-only, sandboxed validator agents. SQA aggregates their judgments under a risk-adaptive quorum predicate that enforces model and archetype diversity, adjusts weights based on calibrated assurance scores, and respects archetype-specific vetoes. Admitted proposals execute only through a sovereign execution gate. We instantiate SQA in a cloud-native control plane and formalize a correlated cognitive failure model for non-deterministic validators. On 500 infrastructure-inspired mutation scenarios, with safety results reported on held-out safe/unsafe trials excluding ambiguous scenarios, SQA reduces unsafe approval from 18.5% for single-agent validation to 0.3% while adding median validation latency of 1.45--4.12 seconds across the studied risk buckets.

</details>


### [332] [CausShield: Sample Reconstruction-Resilient Vertical FL via Causal Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08027)
*Yongqi Jiang,Yansong Gao,Siguang Chen,Anmin Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CausShield是一种基于因果结构模型的垂直联邦学习防御框架，通过将共享表示分解为任务相关和任务无关组件，在不依赖端到端监督训练的情况下实现全周期隐私保护，理论证明其收敛性可保持标准VFL特性，实验表明在隐私保护、模型效用和计算效率上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 垂直联邦学习易受主动样本重建攻击，现有防御方法要么抑制任务相关信息导致模型效用下降，要么依赖端到端监督训练使模型在早期轮次存在脆弱性，无法实现模型效用与隐私保护的良好权衡。

Method: 采用结构因果模型（SCM）视角，将原始样本中的因果特征（任务相关）和非因果特征（任务无关但编码隐私信息）进行理论分离，通过精心设计的无监督表示学习优化问题实现共享表示的分解，并提供收敛性理论保证。

Result: 与包括InvL (USENIX Security'25)和URVFL (NDSS'25)在内的7种SOTA方法对比，CausShield在隐私保护、模型效用和计算效率上均一致性最优。

Conclusion: CausShield通过因果驱动的表示分解策略，在不依赖监督训练的前提下实现了全周期的隐私保护，为VFL中的隐私-效用权衡提供了理论支撑和实用解决方案。

Abstract: Vertical federated learning (VFL) is a distributed learning paradigm that leverages vertically partitioned features across isolated parties without sharing raw samples; however, it remains vulnerable to active sample reconstruction attacks. Existing defenses fail to achieve a satisfactory trade-off between model utility and privacy protection, due to either suppressing task-relevant information alongside privacy-sensitive features or relying on end-to-end supervised training to converge the defense module, which exposes the model to early-epoch vulnerability. To address this challenge, we adopt a structural causal model (SCM) insight and construct CausShield. From a task-learning standpoint, causal features within a raw sample are those that are directly relevant and contributory to the learning objective, whereas non-causal features are task-irrelevant but often encode sample-specific private information, thereby facilitating reconstruction. Importantly, we lay a theoretical foundation to prove this insight. CausShield thus decomposes the shared representations between the client and the coordinating server in VFL into task-relevant and task-irrelevant components to ensure full-cycle privacy protection. Nonetheless, the decomposition is inherently challenging due to the dual objectives of preserving model utility while mitigating privacy leakage. We address this via a carefully formulated optimization problem, which is solved through unsupervised representation learning. We further theoretically prove that CausShield preserves the convergence behavior of standard VFL. Extensive experiments compare CausShield against seven SOTAs, including InvL (USENIX Security'25), and evaluate robustness against advanced reconstruction attacks such as URVFL (NDSS'25). Results demonstrate that CausShield consistently outperforms in privacy protection, model utility, and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [333] [Noise-Adaptive High-Probability Regret Bounds for Online Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08028)
*Wentao Zhang,Yutong Zhang,Wentao Mo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了强凸损失下在线凸优化的高概率遗憾界，在三个方向上取得突破：全信息设置下噪声自适应的遗憾界（超越Azuma-Hoeffding界限）、bandit反馈下高概率遗憾的极小化下界（与全信息形成正式分离）、带随机约束的约束OCO的同时高概率保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决强凸OCO在噪声自适应性、反馈结构和约束满足三个交叉领域中的开放问题，特别是高概率遗憾界在亚高斯随机梯度下的理论最优性，以及不同反馈模型下置信成本的分离。

Method: 提出指数超鞅论证方法绕过Freedman不等式所需的有界差分条件，直接处理无界亚高斯噪声；针对bandit反馈建立极小化下界；结合Slater条件为约束OCO同时提供遗憾和约束违反的高概率保证。

Result: 全信息下噪声自适应遗憾界中鞅偏差项与噪声水平σ成正比而非梯度界G，实现了G/σ的乘性改进；Bandit反馈下高概率遗憾关于log(1/δ)线性增长，与全信息的√log(1/δ)形成分离；约束OCO同时达到O(√T log(m/δ))遗憾和O(√T/(ζδ) + m√T log(m/δ))违反界。

Conclusion: 从理论上刻画了强凸OCO在不同反馈结构和约束条件下的高概率最优遗憾界，揭示了噪声自适应性的本质优势和反馈模型之间的置信成本差异。

Abstract: We study high-probability regret bounds for online convex optimization (OCO) with strongly convex losses and establish three results that resolve open questions at the intersection of noise adaptivity, feedback structure, and constraint satisfaction. For the full-information setting with sub-Gaussian stochastic gradients, we prove a noise-adaptive high-probability regret bound in which the martingale deviation term scales with the noise level $σ$ rather than the gradient bound $G$, yielding a multiplicative improvement of $G/σ$ over the classical Azuma-Hoeffding baseline. Our analysis introduces an exponential supermartingale argument that bypasses the bounded-difference requirement of Freedman's inequality, enabling direct treatment of unbounded sub-Gaussian noise without truncation artifacts. For bandit feedback, we prove a minimax lower bound: the high-probability regret scales linearly in $\log(1/δ)$, in contrast to the $\sqrt{\log(1/δ)}$ confidence cost under full information. This constitutes a formal separation in the confidence cost of strongly convex OCO across feedback models. Regarding constrained OCO with stochastic constraints satisfying a Slater condition, we provide simultaneous high-probability guarantees for both cumulative regret and long-run constraint violation, achieving $\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{T\log(m/δ)})$ regret and $\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{T}/(ζδ) + m\sqrt{T\log(m/δ)})$ violation. Synthetic experiments corroborate all theoretical predictions.

</details>


### [334] [SafeECGMatch: Calibration-Aware Joint Frequency and Time Space Semi-Supervised Learning for Open-Set ECG Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08037)
*Hongkyu Koh,Ikbeom Jang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SafeECGMatch提出了一种校准感知的安全半监督学习框架，用于处理单标签ECG分类中因标签分布不匹配导致的未标记池包含未见类别（OOD异常）问题。该方法通过双分支架构提取时频潜在表征，结合自适应标签平滑和温度缩放进行置信度校准，实现可靠的OOD拒绝和伪标签生成，在PTB-XL和PhysioNet/CinC Challenge基准上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ECG分类模型常面临标签严重稀缺的问题，半监督学习虽有前景但在临床环境中受限：未标记池常包含标记集中未出现的OOD异常或诊断类别，标准SSL会为这些未见类别强制分配错误的伪标签，产生过自信预测。

Method: 提出SafeECGMatch框架：(1) 双分支架构，通过ECG特定数据增强提取时频潜在表征；(2) 自适应标签平滑和温度缩放，动态对齐置信度与经验准确率；(3) 在时域和频域上联合校准多类分类器和OOD检测器，实现可信的OOD拒绝和可靠的伪标签生成。

Result: 在PTB-XL和PhysioNet/CinC Challenge基准上，SafeECGMatch在准确率和校准性能上均达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: SafeECGMatch通过校准感知的安全半监督学习方法，有效解决了标签分布不匹配下的ECG分类问题，推动了生理时间序列数据中可靠知识发现的发展。

Abstract: Electrocardiogram (ECG) classification models often suffer from severe label scarcity, making semi-supervised learning (SSL) an attractive strategy for reducing annotation costs. In clinical settings, however, unlabeled pools frequently contain out-of-distribution (OOD) anomalies or diagnostic groups absent from the labeled set. Standard SSL forces incorrect pseudo-labels onto these unseen classes, producing overconfident predictions. To address this, we propose SafeECGMatch, a calibration-aware safe SSL framework for single-label ECG classification under label distribution mismatch. Methodologically, SafeECGMatch employs a dual-branch architecture extracting time-frequency latent representations via ECG-specific augmentations. Crucially, it dynamically aligns confidence with empirical accuracy through adaptive label smoothing and temperature scaling, calibrating both the multiclass classifier and the OOD detector across temporal and spectral domains. This joint optimization allows trustworthy OOD rejection and reliable pseudo-labeling. Evaluated on the PTB-XL and PhysioNet/CinC Challenge benchmarks, SafeECGMatch achieves state-of-the-art accuracy and calibration, advancing reliable knowledge discovery in physiological time-series. Code is available at https://github.com/labhai/SafeECGMatch.

</details>


### [335] [When Behavioral Safety Evaluation Fails: A Representation-Level Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08044)
*Enyi Jiang,Anders Gjølbye,Yibo Jacky Zhang,Sanmi Koyejo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"审计差距"概念，即LLM行为安全与表征鲁棒性之间的差异，并构建了解离模型（表面安全但潜在空间脆弱），引入潜在脆弱性评分（LVS）评估框架。实验表明，行为安全评估不足以反映模型内部鲁棒性，中间层表征对干预最为敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全评估多集中于行为层面（输出），缺乏对表征层面鲁棒性的考量，导致行为安全与内部脆弱性之间存在未被量化的差距。

Method: 构建"解离模型"（表面安全行为+潜在空间脆弱），提出基于软干预的评估框架（有害微调、逐层潜在扰动），定义潜在脆弱性评分（LVS）量化在有限潜在扰动下诱发有害行为的容易程度。

Result: 解离模型在拒绝行为表现相近的情况下LVS显著升高，中间层表征对干预最为敏感，行为安全指标无法反映表征层面的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 单纯的行为安全评估不足以全面衡量模型鲁棒性，需要同时审计潜在脆弱性和可观测行为。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) safety has often been evaluated at the behavior level, which provides limited evidence of internal robustness, as these evaluations target outputs rather than representation-level vulnerability under intervention. We formalize this discrepancy as the audit gap: the difference between behavioral safety and robustness under intervention. To study this gap, we construct dissociated models that preserve safe outward behavior while remaining vulnerable in the latent space. We introduce an intervention-based evaluation framework to test model robustness through soft interventions in parameter and latent spaces, including harmful fine-tuning and layer-wise latent perturbations. To formalize the evaluation, we propose the Latent Vulnerability Score (LVS) to measure how easily harmful behavior can be elicited by bounded latent perturbations. Using this evaluation framework, we show that behavioral safety metrics are insufficient measures of representation-level robustness across multiple safely and unsafely aligned state-of-the-art models. Notably, dissociated models show substantially elevated LVSs despite comparable refusal behavior under harmful intervention, with intermediate representations being the most sensitive to intervention. Our results suggest that behavioral safety evaluation alone provides an incomplete picture of model robustness, motivating representation-aware audits of latent vulnerability and observable behavior.

</details>


### [336] [Beyond Homophily: Towards Generalized Graph Reconstruction Attack and Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08067)
*Zhanke Zhou,Bo Han,Xuan Li,Jiangchao Yao,Sanmi Koyejo,Michael K. Ng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了图神经网络（GNN）训练邻接矩阵的重构攻击与防御。通过马尔可夫链视角分析GNN推理过程，提出了基于层间表示对齐的MC-GRA(+)攻击方法，以及基于隐私-效用权衡的MC-GPB(+)防御方法。实验表明攻击优于现有方法，防御能在牺牲少量精度的情况下有效降低重构成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN广泛部署于关系数据上，但可能泄露训练图的邻接信息（如社交关系、交易记录等）。现有研究对邻接矩阵在何种条件下可被重构、以及如何有效攻击和防御缺乏系统性理解。

Method: 1. 系统刻画了邻接可恢复性的条件，分析了同质性、异质性和模型归纳偏置的影响。2. 提出马尔可夫链近似视角，将GNN前向计算视为拓扑依赖表示的链式过程。3. 攻击方法MC-GRA(+)：优化替代邻接矩阵，使其各层GNN表示与目标模型对齐。4. 防御方法MC-GPB(+)：在表示链中抑制邻接依赖信息，同时保持分类精度。

Result: 在同质/异质图基准和多种GNN上的实验表明，MC-GRA(+)在重构保真度上优于现有攻击方法；MC-GPB(+)在仅牺牲少量分类精度的条件下，显著降低了重构攻击的成功率。

Conclusion: GNN训练图的邻接信息在不同攻击知识水平下均可被重构，同质性和异质性等图属性会调节泄漏程度。基于马尔可夫链视角的统一攻击-防御框架能有效评估和缓解这一隐私风险，为GNN隐私保护提供了理论支撑和实用方法。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) are widely deployed on relational data, yet they can leak sensitive or proprietary information about the training graph adjacency, e.g., social ties, transactions, and interactions. This work studies graph reconstruction attacks (GRA), a form of model inversion that reconstructs the training adjacency from a trained GNN, given different levels of attacker-side information. We first provide a systematic characterization of when and why adjacency becomes recoverable through features, labels, embeddings, and predictions, with leakage modulated by graph homophily, heterophily, and the model's inductive bias. Motivated by these findings, we view GNN inference through a Markov chain approximation lens, treating the layered forward computation as a chain of topology-dependent representations. Building on this view, we develop complementary attack and defense methods. On the attack side, we propose MC-GRA (+), which reconstructs the adjacency by optimizing a surrogate adjacency whose GNN-induced representations align with those of the target model at each layer. On the defense side, we propose MC-GPB (+), which suppresses adjacency-dependent information throughout the representation chain while aiming to preserve classification accuracy under a privacy-utility trade-off. Experiments across homophilic/heterophilic graph benchmarks and GNNs show that our attacks improve reconstruction fidelity over prior methods, while our defenses reduce reconstruction success with only minor accuracy loss.

</details>


### [337] [DICE: Entropy-Regularized Equilibrium Selection for Stable Multi-Agent LLM Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08068)
*Yi Xie,Zhanke Zhou,Chentao Cao,Bo Liu,Bo Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 多智能体LLM系统常因均衡选择不当而性能不稳，本文引入异质性量化反应均衡（HQRE）作为良定目标，并基于此提出DICE-PC和DICE-FT两种算法，在11个基准测试中分别提升4.3和8.5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统经常无法可靠地超越单个强模型配合best-of-N采样，核心原因在于均衡选择（equilibrium selection）不适定——当前系统只规定了智能体共享哪些信息，但没有规定应选择哪种协调惯例（coordination convention），导致振荡和漂移两种病态现象，引发不稳定学习和线性贝叶斯遗憾。

Method: 将多智能体系统形式化为折扣不完全信息马尔可夫博弈；提出异质性量化反应均衡（HQRE），一种具有智能体与状态相关温度的熵正则化均衡概念；在单调性条件下证明HQRE唯一性、镜像更新线性收敛及有界贝叶斯遗憾；基于此设计DICE-PC（通过提示控制协调冻结模型）和DICE-FT（参数高效镜像微调）两种算法。

Result: 在四个领域11个基准测试中，DICE在准确率-成本权衡上优于同类强基线；推理与规划任务上，DICE-PC平均提升4.3个百分点，DICE-FT提升8.5个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过引入HQRE作为良定均衡目标，能够有效解决多智能体LLM系统中的协调不稳定问题，DICE算法族在多种任务上取得显著提升。

Abstract: Multi-agent large language model (LLM) systems often fail to reliably outperform a single strong model equipped with best-of-N sampling. We argue that a core source of this instability is ill-posed equilibrium selection: current systems specify what information agents share, but not which coordination convention should be selected. We formalize a broad class of such systems as discounted incomplete-information Markov games and show that two common pathologies, oscillation between competing conventions and drift across them, can both induce unstable learning and linear Bayesian regret. To obtain a well-posed target, we introduce the Heterogeneous Quantal Response Equilibrium (HQRE), an entropy-regularized equilibrium concept with agent- and state-dependent temperatures. Under a monotonicity condition, HQRE is unique, admits linearly convergent mirror updates, and yields bounded Bayesian regret; the same condition yields rollout-measurable stability diagnostics. We instantiate this objective in two algorithms: DICE-PC, which coordinates frozen models through prompt-control actions, and DICE-FT, which performs parameter-efficient mirror fine-tuning. Across eleven benchmarks in four domains, DICE improves accuracy-cost trade-offs over strong within-class baselines; on reasoning and planning tasks, DICE-PC improves by 4.3 percentage points on average and DICE-FT by 8.5 points.

</details>


### [338] [ConSteer-RL: Steering Reasoning Capabilities in Large Language Models via Confidence-Aware Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08088)
*Qing Miao,Yiming Zhao,Jing Yang,Chenxi Liu,Yuehai Chen,Yuewen Liu,Shaoyi Du,Badong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ConSteer-RL框架，通过在GRPO中引入基于log-probabilities的token级置信度信号，构建置信度感知奖励函数，惩罚过度自信错误并强化正确自信推理，在多个模型规模上平均提升2.3%-4.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法受限于稀疏二元奖励且忽略模型内在不确定性，需要一种能利用模型置信度信息的更有效的奖励机制。

Method: 基于GRPO框架，将每个token的log-probabilities聚合成标量置信度分数，构建置信度感知奖励，通过基于意识的奖励塑造机制，对过度自信的错误进行惩罚，同时强化正确且自信的推理。

Result: ConSteer-RL在多个模型规模上一致优于强GRPO基线，平均提升2.3%-4.0%。

Conclusion: 将token级置信度信号融入RLVR训练是一种简单有效的改进方法，能显著提升LLM的推理能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has recently become a key paradigm for improving the reasoning abilities of Large Language Models (LLMs), yet it remains limited by sparse binary rewards and its ignorance of model-internal uncertainty. In this paper, we propose ConSteer-RL, a simple yet effective framework that integrates token-level confidence signals derived from model log-probabilities into RLVR training. Specifically, building upon the Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) framework, we construct a confidence-aware reward by aggregating per-token probabilities into a scalar confidence score and incorporating it into an awareness-based reward shaping mechanism that penalizes overconfident errors while reinforcing correct and confident reasoning. Experimental results demonstrate that ConSteer-RL consistently outperforms strong GRPO baselines, achieving average improvements of 2.3%-4.0% across different model scales.

</details>


### [339] [Constraint-Aware Optimization for Robust Protein Stability Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08100)
*A Shivram,Aneesh S. Chivukula,Manik Gupta,Sourav Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需改变SPURS主干架构的约束感知优化框架，通过结合平衡均方误差、Siamese反对称正则化器和基于位置特征的OOD边际一致性损失，在11个基准测试中显著提升了蛋白质突变效应预测的分布外泛化能力，尽管反对称训练并未消除正向-反向偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态ΔΔG预测器在分布内准确率表现良好，但在分布外蛋白质上鲁棒性有限，存在正向-反向偏差且对稀有稳定性突变表征不足。现有方法主要通过增加架构组件来解决这些问题，而优化层面的干预相对未被充分探索。

Method: 提出了约束感知优化框架，包含三个核心组件：平衡均方误差（Balanced MSE）、Siamese反对称正则化器（Siamese anti-symmetric regularizer），以及基于逐位置特征表示的新型OOD边际一致性损失（OOD-margin consistency loss）。该框架不需要对SPURS骨干网络进行任何架构修改。

Result: 在11个基准测试和3个随机种子上的实验表明：S669上的Spearman相关系数从0.486提升至0.540（种子间σ=0.002），匹配了已发表的SPURS基线（0.50）；S461上从0.653提升至0.711；在五个额外的OOD数据集上取得一致性的较小增益。

Conclusion: Ssym上的受控诊断分析表明，反对称训练并未消除系统性正向-反向偏差，这表明性能提升源于隐式正则化（implicit regularization）而非精确的热力学约束强制执行。

Abstract: Multimodal $ΔΔG$ predictors integrating protein language models with inverse-folding representations achieve strong in-distribution accuracy on the Megascale dataset but exhibit limited robustness on out-of-distribution (OOD) proteins, persistent forward-reverse bias on paired-mutation benchmarks, and under-representation of rare stabilizing mutations. Existing approaches address these limitations primarily through additional architectural components, leaving optimization-level intervention comparatively underexplored. We introduce a constraint-aware optimization framework combining Balanced Mean Squared Error, a Siamese anti-symmetric regularizer, and a novel OOD-margin consistency loss on the per-position feature representation, requiring no architectural changes to the SPURS backbone. Across eleven benchmarks and three random seeds, the framework improves Spearman correlation on S669 from 0.486 to 0.540 ($σ=0.002$ across seeds), matching the published SPURS baseline (0.50) without architectural modification, and on S461 from 0.653 to 0.711, with consistent smaller gains on five additional OOD datasets. A controlled diagnostic on Ssym reveals that anti-symmetric training does not eliminate systematic forward-reverse bias, indicating that gains arise through implicit regularization rather than exact thermodynamic constraint enforcement.

</details>


### [340] [A Unifying View of Attention Sinks: Two Algorithms, Two Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08105)
*Lukas Fesser,Mozes Jacobs,Thomas Fel,Andy Keller,Sham Kakade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 注意力坍塌到单一token（sink）时，可能对应两种不同机制：自适应空操作（nop）和广播（broadcast）。两者视觉相似但计算本质不同，现有干预方法（gating/registers）分别隐含地针对其中一种机制，而实际模型中两种机制并存，需组合干预才能有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力sink现象在softmax transformer中普遍存在，但视觉上相同的sink模式可能掩盖了根本不同的算法。先前工作对sink的成因和功能理解不一，缺乏对底层机制的区分，导致干预方法可能错误匹配。

Method: 通过合成任务形式化区分nop sink（值范数可忽略）和broadcast sink（输出低秩）；利用这些诊断工具分析预训练视觉Transformer中sink的分布与演变；对比gating（隐式假设nop）和register tokens（隐式假设broadcast）两种干预方法的效果。

Result: 两种机制在规模上并存：sink从早期层的CLS标记向深层patch标记转移，集中在特定注意力头。register tokens本为broadcast设计，却被模型同时用于nop，单一干预均不充分；组合gating与registers在稳定性和性能上取得互补增益。

Conclusion: 相同的注意力sink模式可反映两种截然不同的计算；有效的干预需要先理解模型实际在计算什么，而非盲目假设单一机制。

Abstract: When attention concentrates on a single token, a sink, what is the model actually computing? Attention sinks are ubiquitous in softmax transformers, yet this shared visual signature can hide fundamentally different algorithms. We show that visually similar sink patterns can reflect two distinct mechanisms: {i} adaptive nop, where a head suppresses its update by routing to a null token, and {ii} broadcast, where a sink aggregates and redistributes global information. In that case, sinks serve an analogous role: a safe destination when there is nothing useful to compute. Proposed interventions like gating or registers work because they implicitly target one or the other, revealing a duality between method and assumed mechanism: gating implicitly assumes nop; registers implicitly assume broadcast. Each mechanism leaves distinct traces (nop sinks exhibit negligible value norms; broadcast sinks induce low-rank outputs) which we formalize on synthetic tasks and use to derive practical diagnostics. Applied to pretrained vision transformers, these diagnostics reveal that both mechanisms exist at scale: sinks transition from CLS in early layers to patches in deeper layers, and concentrate in specialized heads. Strikingly, register tokens, designed for broadcast, are repurposed to also serve nop, confirming that neither intervention alone suffices. Combining gating with registers yields complementary gains in stability and performance. Overall, we find that the same attention pattern can reflect two very different computations and effective intervention requires first asking what the model is actually computing.

</details>


### [341] [Conditional Random Ordered Transport Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08113)
*Lei Luo,Jian Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出条件随机有序传输空间（CROTS），一种基于L^0值的随机概率测度空间理论，通过引入有序传输差异和条件风险泛函，在Wasserstein度量的基础上附加方向性序约束，为证据受限、因果、单调等可信分布学习提供数学框架。理论成果包括有序传输的对偶性、变分收敛、测度论完备性、约束重心、条件风险-传输对偶以及稳定性定理，后者揭示了Wasserstein收敛与局部序违规风险之间的分离递归结构，为分布偏移与鲁棒性失效提供了数学语言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的Wasserstein距离仅度量概率分布之间的差异大小，但无法判断质量移动的方向是否满足语义、因果、物理、单调或风险敏感等约束。在证据受限学习中，不仅需要知道两个分布相距多远，更需要知道质量是否沿着信息允许的方向移动。现有方法如锥值度量、有序Kantorovich构造、随机Wasserstein空间等均无法同时处理随机性、序结构和条件证据信息，因此亟需一种新的空间理论。

Method: 定义CROTS为L^0值随机概率测度空间，配备Wasserstein环境度量、封闭随机序、硬/软有序传输差异以及条件风险泛函。硬有序传输施加严格序约束，软有序传输引入惩罚项允许轻微违规。通过变分收敛建立软到硬的极限联系，利用测度论方法证明随机提升空间的完备性，通过条件风险-传输对偶刻画序违规程度，并建立稳定性定理揭示环境Wasserstein收敛与局部序违规风险递归的分离性。

Result: 建立了CROTS的完整理论基础：有序传输的适定性与对偶性、软到硬的变分收敛、随机提升空间的Lebesgue-Bochner可测性与完备性、退化为经典Wasserstein和有序几何的条件、有序测地线、约束重心与投影、条件风险-传输对偶、序违规分布的可分离性，以及主稳定性定理。

Conclusion: CROTS理论为证据过度扩展、有序分布偏移、鲁棒性失效和可容许分布动力学提供了严格的数学语言。其核心洞察在于：模型可以在Wasserstein度量下收敛，但其局部可容许性泄漏遵循独立的序风险递归，最终的渐近序风险下界成为衡量学习过程可靠性的关键指标。

Abstract: A small Wasserstein distance does not certify that a transformation is admissible. In evidence-constrained, semantic, causal, physical, monotone, or risk-sensitive learning, one must ask not only how far two probability laws are, but whether mass has moved in a direction allowed by available information. We introduce conditional random ordered transport spaces (CROTS), a class of \(L^0\)-valued spaces of random probability measures equipped with a Wasserstein ambient metric, a closed stochastic order, hard and soft ordered transport discrepancies, and a conditional risk functional for evaluating order violation under an evidence sigma-field. The central object is an order-admissible transport geometry for random measure-valued dynamics, distinct from cone-valued metrics, ordered Kantorovich constructions, random Wasserstein spaces alone, and model-specific residuals for generative paths. We develop the foundations of CROTS as a space theory for reliable distributional learning. The results include well-posedness and duality for hard and soft ordered transport, soft-to-hard variational convergence, measurability and completeness of the random lifted space, reductions to classical Wasserstein and ordered geometries, ordered geodesics, constrained barycenters and projections, conditional risk-transport duality, and separation of order-violating distributions. The main stability theorem shows that random learning dynamics may converge in the ambient Wasserstein metric while its local admissibility leakage follows a separate conditional order-risk recursion. The resulting asymptotic order-risk floor provides a mathematical language for evidence overreach, ordered distribution shift, robustness failure, and admissible distributional dynamics.

</details>


### [342] [TRUST-SCF: Transformer-based Risk Understanding and Scoring for Transactional Supply Chain Finance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08140)
*Mohammadamin Davoodabadi,Amirabbas Shakeri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRUST-SCF是一个基于Transformer的供应链金融信用评分框架，通过交易级风险预测和动态信用评分，在30万+真实交易数据上优于序列基线模型，并能有效预测未来还款行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 供应链金融和LendTech平台需要能够响应交易行为变化、还款延迟和活跃敞口的信用评分系统，现有方法难以捕捉交易序列中的动态模式。

Method: (1) 金融对齐的注意力偏置，结合利用率相似性和时间近因；(2) 对数变换空间中的连续还款延迟预测；(3) 无需外部信用评分标签的标签高效信用评分流程，通过预测延迟、模拟利用率下的潜在风险、实际未偿敞口和非线性校准推导最终评分。

Result: 在30万+交易的真实数据上，TRUST-SCF在延迟预测上优于序列基线模型，且生成的评分与未来还款行为强相关。

Conclusion: TRUST-SCF是适用于SCF和LendTech环境的自适应信用评分和交易级风险缓解的实用框架。

Abstract: Supply Chain Finance (SCF) and LendTech platforms need credit scoring systems that respond to evolving transaction behavior, repayment delays, and active exposure. We propose TRUST-SCF, a transformer-based framework for transaction-level risk prediction and dynamic credit scoring. Each user history is represented as a sequence of transaction tokens containing utilization, repayment delay and transaction position. The main contributions are: (1) a financially aligned attention bias that combines utilization similarity and recency, enabling the model to compare repayment behavior under comparable exposure conditions; (2) continuous repayment-delay prediction in a log-transformed target space, reducing the influence of extreme delays while improving sensitivity to short-delay behavior and (3) a label-efficient credit-scoring pipeline in which the final credit score is not trained using any explicit external credit-score label, but is instead derived from predicted delay, potential risk over simulated utilization, actual unpaid exposure, and nonlinear calibration. Experiments on real transaction data from more than 300,000 transactions show that TRUST-SCF improves delay prediction over sequential baselines and produces scores that are strongly associated with future repayment behavior. These results suggest that TRUST-SCF is a practical framework for adaptive credit scoring and transaction-level risk mitigation in SCF and LendTech environments.

</details>


### [343] [LogNEO: A GPT-Neo Reinforcement Learning Framework for Accurate Real-Time Log Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08153)
*David Eje,Tanmay Sharma,Khush Patel,Manuel Mazzara,Leonard Johard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LogNEO是基于GPT-Neo (1.3B)的日志异常检测器，采用部分信用指数衰减的位置感知奖励和交叉熵正则化的PPO算法微调。在HDFS、BGL和Thunderbird基准上分别达到0.927、0.913和0.984的F1分数，召回率比先前最优LogGPT提升最多6个百分点；生产级微服务部署（Kafka+Redis+TensorRT加速）实现45ms端到端延迟，吞吐量15,000事件/秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模系统日志中存在大量正常与异常模式，现有方法对日志序列中位置相关的预测难度建模不足，导致异常检测召回率偏低。LogGPT等先前方法未区分不同位置的预测难度差异，难以充分捕捉长序列中早期与后期日志的预测偏差。

Method: 基于EleutherAI的GPT-Neo（1.3B参数）作为骨干网络，采用近端策略优化（PPO）进行微调；提出部分信用指数衰减位置感知奖励机制，对早期位置正确预测给予更高奖励，对后期位置错误预测施加更强惩罚，同时结合交叉熵正则化防止过拟合。

Result: 在HDFS、BGL、Thunderbird三个日志基准上F1分数分别为0.927、0.913和0.984；相较于LogGPT，召回率提升最多6个百分点，同时保持相当水平的精确率。生产部署下端到端延迟45ms，吞吐量达15,000事件/秒。

Conclusion: LogNEO通过强化学习引导的位置感知奖励建模，有效提升了日志异常检测的召回率与整体F1分数，且在实际生产环境中具备低延迟、高吞吐的部署可行性。

Abstract: Detecting anomalies in large-scale system logs is critical for the reliability and security of modern computing infrastructure. We present LogNEO, a log anomaly detector built on EleutherAI's GPT-Neo (1.3B parameters) and fine-tuned with a novel partial-credit, exponentially decaying position-aware reward scheme combined with cross-entropy regularisation via Proximal Policy Optimisation (PPO). The position-aware reward explicitly models prediction difficulty: early positions receive higher rewards for correct predictions, while later positions incur stronger penalties for errors. LogNEO attains F1-scores of 0.927, 0.913, and 0.984 on the HDFS, BGL, and Thunderbird benchmarks, improving recall by up to 6 percentage points over the prior state-of-the-art LogGPT while maintaining comparable precision. A production microservice deployment over Apache Kafka, Redis, and TensorRT-accelerated inference demonstrates 45 ms end-to-end latency at 15,000 events per second.

</details>


### [344] [Have I Solved This Before? Retrieving Similar Segmentation Problems for Evolutionary Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08155)
*Andreas Margraf,Henning Cui,Jörg Hähner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种偏离传统监控系统开发流程的研究方向，通过逐步收集知识并存储在抽象系统模型中，实现过滤管线的跨域迁移和复用，避免从零开始训练模型，并以图像分割问题为例进行了统计分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代制造环境中监控系统的可靠集成与配置是实现高效率和高生产率的基本前提，但传感器类型和系统架构的设计决策需在早期且不确定性较高的情况下做出。传统开发流程侧重于算法设计，导致重复训练成本高、后期修改风险大，亟需一种更深入分析检测问题本身的方法。

Method: 偏离传统监控系统开发流程，将关注点从算法设计转向对检测问题的深入分析；逐步收集知识并存储在抽象系统模型中，实现类似解决方案的检索和复用；分析过滤管线的跨域迁移潜力，将其迁移到不同但相似的分割问题中；采用统计分析方法评估这种迁移学习变体的效果。

Result: 通过统计分析验证了过滤管线跨域迁移的可行性；发现简单模型有助于平衡复杂度、技术要求和可靠性之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 复用先前生成的管线可降低后期昂贵修改的风险；增量式优化现有基础配置比从头训练模型更高效；跨域迁移过滤管线在图像分割问题中具有潜力。

Abstract: Reliable integration and solid configuration of monitoring systems constitute a fundamental prerequisites for achieving high efficiency and productivity in contemporary manufacturing environments. Design decisions on sensor type and system architecture have to be made at an early stage and under comparably high uncertainty. This work investigates a research direction that deviates from the traditional monitoring-system development process by shifting the attention from algorithm design to a deeper analysis of the inspection problem. In contrast to traditional design cycles, this paper proposes to gradually collect knowledge and store it in an abstract system model. This enables the retrieval of similar solutions for future use cases, preventing the need for expensive model training from scratch and allowing instead for the incremental refinement of existing base configurations. Reuse of previously generated pipelines reduces the risk of late and costly revisions. As there is little knowledge on cross-domain transferability of filter pipelines, this study analyzes the potential of retrieving filter pipelines to transfer them to different but similar segmentation problems. Finally, we statistically analyze the benefits of this `transfer learning' variant which is predominantly applied to image segmentation problems. In addition, we discuss how simple models help balancing the trade-off between complexity, technical requirements, and reliability in the design process.

</details>


### [345] [AttentionCap: Transformer Based Capacitance Matrix Learning Toward Full-Chip Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08161)
*Jiechen Huang,Hector R. Rodriguez,Dingcheng Yang,Zuochang Ye,Yibo Lin,Wenjian Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AttentionCap，一种基于Transformer的电容矩阵学习模型，利用注意力机制与电容矩阵的相似性，通过Gram表示框架、对称注意力输出层和归一化拉普拉斯损失实现多节点、多层的电容提取，在未见过的真实设计上达到0.67%/3.99%的自/耦合电容误差，相比CNN-Cap降低4.6×/5.7×误差并实现192×推理加速，且能高效迁移到新工艺节点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于规则的模板匹配电容提取在先进节点下精度难以维持，而现有MLP和CNN方法受限于固定金属层组合和特定工艺节点，实用性受限。

Method: 提出AttentionCap Transformer模型：1) Gram表示框架用于电容矩阵学习；2) 物理对齐的对称注意力输出层；3) 归一化拉普拉斯损失函数；4) 工艺节点嵌入支持多节点学习。

Result: 在多层级多节点设定下，对未见过的真实设计自电容误差0.67%、耦合电容误差3.99%；相比CNN-Cap基线，自/耦合误差降低4.6×/5.7×，推理速度提升192×；预训练模型仅需5K样本和4K微调步数即可准确迁移到未见过的节点。

Conclusion: AttentionCap在未见过的真实设计上具有足够精度，且对新工艺节点具有强迁移能力，为现代EDA工作流提供了高度实用价值。

Abstract: As capacitance extraction accuracy of rule-based pattern matching becomes difficult to sustain at advanced nodes, a growing trend emerges to develop deep-learning-based 2D capacitance models. However, existing MLP- and CNN-based methods constrain their input to fixed metal-layer combinations in a specific process node, limiting their usability in practice. Recognizing the inherent similarity between capacitance matrix and the prevailing attention mechanism, we propose AttentionCap, a customized Transformer for capacitance matrix learning, with a Gram representation framework, a physics-aligned symmetric-attention output layer, and a novel normalized Laplacian loss. We also introduce a process-node embedding to enable multi-node learning. Trained on synthetic data, AttentionCap attains 0.67\%/3.99\% self/coupling-capacitance error on unseen real designs under a multi-layer and multi-node setting, surpassing the CNN-Cap baseline with 4.6$\times$/5.7$\times$ lower self/coupling error and 192$\times$ faster inference speed. A pretrained AttentionCap accurately transfers to an unseen node with only 5K samples and 4K finetuning steps. With sufficient accuracy on unseen real designs and strong transferability to new process nodes, AttentionCap offers highly practical value for modern EDA workflows. Code and data are available at https://github.com/THU-numbda/AttentionCap.

</details>


### [346] [Explaining Data Mixing Scaling Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08167)
*Rui Dai,Shuran Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的理论框架来解释多领域数据混合中的缩放定律，通过容量竞争和噪声减少两个关键机制，在更少参数下实现了比现有经验定律更好的损失拟合和跨尺度外推能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多领域数据混合缩放定律主要基于经验拟合，缺乏理论理解。本文旨在填补这一空白，从理论上解释混合数据训练中模型损失行为的内在机制。

Method: 将标准神经缩放定律（如Kaplan和Chinchilla）的理论视角扩展到多领域场景。基于领域间共享基础技能、差异专长技能的分布假设，识别出两大关键因素：容量竞争（有限模型容量在全局范围内耦合领域损失）和噪声减少（最优权重向更难学习的领域偏移以最小化整体噪声）。

Result: 该框架在拟合损失曲面时平均相对误差更低，能识别出更高性能的训练混合方案。成功实现了跨尺度外推，使用小尺度拟合的参数即可预测大尺度的最优数据混合。相比先前经验定律，参数数量显著更少。

Conclusion: 提出的统一理论框架不仅更准确地解释了多领域数据混合的缩放行为，而且具有更强的可泛化性和参数效率，为数据混合策略的优化提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Recent research has established empirical scaling laws to predict model performance on multi-domain data mixtures. However, a theoretical understanding of these model loss behaviors remains absent. In this work, we propose a unified framework to explain the underlying mechanics of data mixing. Our approach extends theoretical perspectives originally developed for standard neural scaling laws (e.g., Kaplan and Chinchilla) to the multi-domain setting. Based on the distributional assumption that domains overlap on fundamental skills while diverging on specialized skills, we identify two key factors that govern the domain losses of models trained on different data mixtures: \textit{Capacity Competition}, where the allocation of finite model capacity couples domain losses globally, and \textit{Noise Reduction}, where optimal weights shift toward harder-to-learn domains to minimize overall noise. Empirical evaluations show that our framework outperforms existing baselines by fitting the loss landscape with a lower Mean Relative Error and identifying higher-performing training mixtures. Most importantly, our model successfully extrapolates across scales, predicting highly effective mixtures for large, unseen scales using parameters fitted on smaller ones. In addition, our model achieves these results using significantly fewer parameters compared to previous empirical laws. Our code is available at https://github.com/meiqwq/Explaining-Data-Mixing-Scaling-Laws.

</details>


### [347] [Frequency-Domain Latent Attention Gating for Cross-Domain Token Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08191)
*Kewei Li,Rongying Zhang,Xueli Wang,Xiwen Gong,Zhongjian Wang,Lan Huang,Ruochi Zhang,Fengfeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FLaG模块，利用FFT将token变换到频域，通过可学习潜向量进行频谱聚合与通道门控，再重建回时域进行池化。在AMP活性预测、图像分类（CIFAR-10/100）、文本分类（IMDB/GLUE）上验证，尤其在AMP和CIFAR-100上增益明显。进一步通过频带消融、门控分析、残基扰动等探针实验揭示低频贡献最大、高频更具样本特异性等行为特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Token聚合（池化）是将token表示映射到样本级预测的常见瓶颈，现有池化方法大多仅在原始token域操作，缺乏对频域信息的利用。

Method: 提出FLaG插件模块：1) 用实FFT将token表示变换到频域；2) 用可学习潜向量（latent queries）汇总频谱分量；3) 应用通道级门控（channel-wise gate）进行频谱重加权；4) 重建增强的时域token用于最终池化。

Result: 在ESM2-8M抗菌肽任务和CIFAR-100上取得最明显增益，在IMDB和GLUE上与强文本基线持平。探针实验表明低频贡献最大、高频模式更具样本特异性，门控是广泛共享的频谱重加权阶段，交叉注意力模式具有样本特异性，高螺旋肽在两种细菌中均表现出更强的平均频谱敏感性。

Conclusion: FLaG通过频域操作有效提升了token聚合能力，尤其在中等规模数据和生物序列任务上优势突出，揭示了频谱池化中低频主导、高频特异的行为模式。

Abstract: Token aggregation is a common bottleneck in models that map token representations to sample-level predictions, yet most pooling methods operate only in the original token domain. We propose FLaG, a plug-in aggregation module that transforms token representations with the real FFT, summarizes spectral components with learnable latent queries, applies a channel-wise gate, and reconstructs enhanced time-domain tokens for final pooling. We evaluate FLaG on antimicrobial peptide (AMP) activity prediction with ESM2, image classification with ResNet18 on CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100, and text classification with RoBERTa on IMDB and GLUE. FLaG achieves its clearest gains on the ESM2-8M antimicrobial peptide tasks and on CIFAR-100, while remaining competitive with strong text baselines on IMDB and GLUE. Then we probe its behavior on the AMP setting with band knockouts, gate summaries, residue perturbations, latent-query readouts, and structure-proxy stratification. We find that low-frequency bands contribute the most overall, and the remaining higher-band pattern is more sample-specific. The gate acts as a broadly shared spectral reweighting stage and the cross-attention patterns are sample-specific with mild query-wise differentiation, and higher-helix peptides exhibit stronger average spectral sensitivity in both bacteria. The supplementary materials, source code and data are released at https://www.healthinformaticslab.org/supp/ and https://github.com/Kewei2023/AMPCliff/tree/FLaG.

</details>


### [348] [Neural Field Tokenizations with Hierarchy and Spatial Locality Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08204)
*Alonso Urbano,David W. Romero,Max Zimmer,Sebastian Pokutta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LH-NeF框架，通过局部保持的分层编码器将连续信号映射为结构化token表示，以单次前向传播替代元学习的内循环优化，在保持模态无关性的同时大幅提升效率（内存减少42倍，批量大小提升133倍），在图像、3D形状和气候场等模态上重建与下游任务性能达到或超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经场表示学习方法中，基于样本的元学习因内存密集型内循环优化而扩展性差；前馈编码方式则通常引入模态特定假设，牺牲了神经场的通用性。需要一种既保持模态无关性又具备高效可扩展性的表示学习范式。

Method: 提出LH-NeF框架：采用局部保持的分层编码器，将原始坐标-值的场观测映射为结构化token序列；训练时从这些token中重建场数据；用单次前向传播替代元学习的内循环优化。

Result: 相比最强的模态无关基线，LH-NeF内存使用减少42倍，批量大小提升133倍；在图像、3D形状和气候场三个模态上，重建和下游任务性能均匹配或超越模态无关、模态特定及专用生成式神经场基线。

Conclusion: 局部性和层次性是学习神经场表示的有效先验，可在不牺牲模态无关性的前提下显著提升效率和性能，为神经场的通用表示学习提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Neural fields parameterize data as functions from coordinates to values, providing a unified framework for representation learning across modalities. Existing approaches are dominated by per-sample meta-learning, which scales poorly due to memory-intensive inner-loop optimization. The natural alternative -- feed-forward encoding -- typically introduces modality-specific assumptions, sacrificing the generality that makes learning with neural fields attractive. We argue that locality and hierarchy are useful priors for learning field representations that can be injected without compromising modality-agnosticism. We propose LH-NeF, a framework to learn general-purpose tokenized representations of continuous signals. A locality-preserving hierarchical encoder maps raw coordinate-value field observations to structured tokens, from which the field is reconstructed during training. By replacing meta-learning's inner loop with a single forward pass, LH-NeF uses 42$\times$ less memory and supports 133$\times$ larger batches than the strongest modality-agnostic baseline. Across images, 3D shapes, and climate fields, our learned representations match or exceed performance of modality-agnostic, modality-specific, and specialized generative neural field baselines on both reconstruction and downstream tasks.

</details>


### [349] [Public Machine Learning Solver Framework for Novices in the Machine Learning Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08212)
*Lokman Saleh,Hafedh Mili,Mounir Boukadoum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向非专家的半自动化机器学习流水线推荐平台，结合专家定义的算法选择准则与迁移学习，自动提取数据特征，通过一阶逻辑推理推荐完整流水线，并集成众包机制持续更新知识库。这是首个免费、公开的、系统化捕获专家知识以指导非专家解决机器学习问题的在线框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决机器学习问题通常需要专业知识，现有系统虽已出现但仍有局限：完全自动化系统缺乏透明度，专家速查表过于简化，决策支持系统推荐单一算法而非完整流水线。因此需要一种既能保留专家知识又能智能推荐完整解决方案的平台，以帮助非专家用户。

Method: 平台结合了类别2（专家速查表）和类别3（基于选择准则的决策支持系统），采用半自动化方式。具体方法包括：(1) 使用专家定义的算法选择准则与迁移学习；(2) 自动从用户提供的数据集中提取数据特征（如类别不平衡、缺失值）；(3) 基于一阶逻辑对知识库进行推理，推荐按相关性排序的算法；(4) 提供用户友好界面并连接到众包平台，由ML专家持续更新知识库；(5) 采用增量式构建，支持新算法、准则和领域知识的无缝集成。

Result: 该平台能推荐完整的机器学习流水线（而非单一算法），按相关性排序推荐算法，并且是首个免费、公开可访问的在线框架。平台可持续更新，支持新知识的增量集成。

Conclusion: 本文提出的平台填补了现有AutoML系统与专家知识之间的空白，为非专家用户提供了一种结构化的、透明的机器学习问题解决途径，同时保持了系统的可扩展性和持续更新能力。

Abstract: Solving machine learning problems is complex and typically reserved for experts. Over the past two decades, systems have emerged to support non-experts. Based on our review, we identify three categories: (1) fully automated AutoML systems, (2) expert cheat sheets for algorithm selection, and (3) decision-support systems using selection criteria (accuracy, transparency, data requirements). We propose a new platform combining categories 2 and 3 to deliver semi-automated, intelligent solution recommendations for non-experts. Unlike existing approaches that recommend a single algorithm, our platform suggests a complete pipeline tailored to the user's problem. It integrates expert-defined selection criteria with transfer learning and automatically extracts data characteristics (e.g., class imbalance, missing values) from user-provided datasets. The platform uses first-order logic to reason over its knowledge base and recommends suitable algorithms ranked by relevance. It features a user-friendly interface and connects to a crowdsourcing platform for ML experts, ensuring continuous updates. The platform is built incrementally, allowing seamless integration of new algorithms, criteria, and domain knowledge. To our knowledge, this is the first free, publicly accessible online framework that systematically captures and operationalizes expert knowledge to guide non-experts in solving ML problems in a structured, transparent manner.

</details>


### [350] [How Deep Are Deep GPs, Really? A Sharp Threshold and a Non-Gaussian Limit for Compositional GPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08218)
*Mark Kozdoba,Shie Mannor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了深度高斯过程（Deep Gaussian Process）在先验分布随层数增加时的极限行为。对于RBF核，当带宽参数r超过阈值r_c(d) = Θ(√d)时，先验退化为常数函数（退化分布）；当r低于该阈值时，先验收敛到非退化、非高斯的极限分布π_Ż，且坐标间存在非零依赖。实验验证了该阈值并展示了极限分布的多模态行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解深度贝叶斯模型中组合先验（compositional priors）随深度增加的极限行为，特别是探索非退化、非平凡的极限分布是否存在及其性质，以弥补先前仅知道退化极限的不足。

Method: 理论分析：建立RBF核下带宽r的尖锐阈值r_c(d)=Θ(√d)，证明低于阈值时先验收敛到非退化、非高斯极限分布π_Ż。实验验证：在不同维度d下验证阈值，并分析极限分布的多模态特性。

Result: 1. 确定了RBF核下带宽的尖锐阈值r_c(d)=Θ(√d)；2. 证明了低于阈值时先验收敛到非退化、非高斯的极限分布π_Ż；3. 实验显示极限分布具有复杂多模态行为，且该区域随维度增加变窄。

Conclusion: 深度高斯过程先验在合适带宽下可以存在非平凡的极限分布，打破了以往仅知退化极限的认知，为深度贝叶斯模型的设计提供了新的理论指导。

Abstract: Compositional priors describe the generic properties of layered functions in deep Bayesian models, where deep neural networks with random weights are a canonical example.In the wide-network limit, the prior is a Gaussian process with a depth-dependent kernel, and its behaviour as depth grows has been extensively studied through this kernel. Here, we study another case, where each layer itself is a vector valued Gaussian process, and our aim is similarly to understand the limiting behaviour of the prior as depth grows.
  Previous GP work has established that for the RBF kernel and a certain range of bandwidths $r$, the prior degenerates in the limit, converging to the set of constant functions -- which is not useful as a probabilistic model. In this paper we establish several new results. First, we identify a sharp bandwidth threshold $r_c(d) = Θ(\sqrt{d})$ above which the limit is degenerate, strengthening the earlier bounds. Second, and more importantly, we show that for $r$ below the threshold $r_c(d)$ the prior converges to a limit distribution $π_{\bar{Z}}$. We also prove that these distributions are non-degenerate and non-Gaussian, with non-vanishing dependence between coordinates. In contrast to the previously known degenerate regime, deep Gaussian process priors can therefore admit non-trivial limits.
  Empirically, we verify the threshold across a range of dimensions $d$, and demonstrate a complex multimodal behaviour of the limit distributions $π_{\bar{Z}}$ -- a regime that becomes increasingly narrow with $d$ and would be hard to identify without knowing the threshold.

</details>


### [351] [De novo molecular generation with optical property preconditioning at the token level](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08221)
*Haozhe Huang,Manuel Gonzalez Lastre,Hyun Suk Park,Jorge A. Campos-Gonzalez-Angulo,Xinjian Liu,Alán Aspuru-Guzik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在低数据条件下，采用基于GPT2的标记条件自回归语言模型生成OLED分子，通过离散属性token实现条件控制，并利用TDDFT评估光学性质。结果表明模型能复现训练分布的主要光学性质，但控制能力在不同化学亚结构上存在显著差异：芳香族共轭体系控制较好，而含吸电子基团（特别是芳基腈）的分子则系统性红移且可控性下降。研究强调需在化学子空间而非聚合分布上评估模型可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OLED分子设计中高质量数据稀缺，现有生成模型在化学基序上的条件控制可靠性不足，亟需建立低数据条件下的可控生成基准。

Method: 采用GPT2模型在大规模化学语料上预训练，加入离散属性token后进行多任务微调，条件目标为垂直吸收能和振子强度，辅以HOMO-LUMO能隙作为电子描述符，生成分子通过TDDFT评估分布保真度和可控性。

Result: 生成库复现了训练分布的主要光学性质，但倾向于更低分子量和更少重原子。token级控制在条件区间内方向一致但非完全正交，局部校准存在不规则性。含吸电子基团（芳基腈）的分子系统性红移且可控性降低，而中等共轭芳香碳基团则联合目标满足率更高。

Conclusion: 建立了条件OLED分子生成的定量基准，并指出模型的可靠性必须在化学子空间而非聚合分布上评估。

Abstract: Designing OLED molecules with targeted optical properties remains challenging due to the scarcity of high-quality data and the limited reliability of conditional control in generative models across chemical motifs. Here, we benchmark a token-conditioned autoregressive language model for OLED molecular generation in a realistic low-data regime. A GPT2 model is pretrained on large chemical corpora, augmented with discrete property tokens, and fine-tuned using multi-task optimisation. Conditioning targets vertical absorption energy and oscillator strength, with the HOMO-LUMO gap included as an auxiliary electronic descriptor.
  Generated molecules are evaluated at the TDDFT level to assess distributional fidelity and controllability. The generated library reproduces the dominant optical-property support of the training distribution while shifting towards lower molecular weight and fewer heavy atoms. Token-level control is consistently directional across conditioning bins, but is not fully orthogonal and exhibits local calibration irregularities. A chemotype-resolved analysis further shows that controllability depends strongly on local electronic environments: moderately conjugated aromatic-carbon motifs are associated with improved joint target satisfaction, whereas electron-withdrawing motifs, particularly aryl nitriles, show systematic red-shifting and reduced controllability.
  These results establish a quantitative benchmark for conditional OLED molecular generation and show that model reliability must be assessed in chemically meaningful subspaces rather than from aggregate property distributions alone.

</details>


### [352] [GPT-Micro: A large language paradigm for accelerated, inexpensive, and thermodynamics-consistent discovery of constitutive models in manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08238)
*Soumik Dutta,Kiarash Naghavi Khanghah,Sania Shree,Logan McNeil,Thomas Feldhausen,Hongyi Xu,Rajiv Malhotra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GPT-Micro范式，利用大语言模型（LLM）自主发现热力学兼容的构型模型，在印刷电子工艺测试中实现>70%数据减负、400倍发现时间缩短，并生成全新函数形式的可解释模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统构型建模依赖人类专家经验，模型发现过程缓慢且增量式；常规机器学习数据生成成本高；现有LLM方法忽略热力学基本定律。

Method: GPT-Micro框架：整合文献语义知识提取、基于热力学的守恒定律强制执行、稀疏数据集，结合LLM驱动的模型假设生成与迭代优化。

Result: (a) 相比ML方法数据负担降低>70%且精度无损；(b) 相比人工驱动建模发现时间从数月缩短至数小时（400倍加速）；(c) 发现全新函数形式的模型，无需主观初始假设；(d) 生成紧凑、守恒合规、物理完备的分析模型，提升可解释性与机理洞察。

Conclusion: GPT-Micro范式有望在制造领域实现快速、低成本、物理可信且可解释的微观结构建模。

Abstract: Constitutive modeling of the relationship between process-imposed material states and fundamental material properties is critical to control of material microstructure in manufacturing processes. The limited accuracy resulting from the typical reliance on fallible human expertise and intuition for postulation and revision of the models functional form results in incremental and time consuming model discovery. Conventional Machine Learning (ML) incurs significant cost and time of data generation. Model discovery using Large Language Models (LLMs) suffers from the above issues and/or ignores the inviolability of fundamental thermodynamics laws. This work creates a novel GPT-Micro paradigm for autonomous, data sparse, and thermodynamics-compliant discovery of de-novo constitutive models. This framework seamlessly integrates semantic knowledge extraction from literature, enforcement of thermodynamics-based conservation laws, and sparse datasets, with LLM-driven generation and refinement of model hypotheses. Validation is performed for a long-intractable constitutive modeling problem in a printed electronics process testbed.
